":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"3076679d-2588-40d5-8e56-1ccafb4e512d","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"a7d6a163-b014-4253-accb-0f72dad95e80","timestamp":"2026-05-01T12:45:18.244Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"a7d6a163-b014-4253-accb-0f72dad95e80","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring or one-time remote agent (\"run this every Monday\", \"open a cleanup PR for X in 2 weeks\"), or to manage existing routines. ALSO OFFER PROACTIVELY: after you finish work that has a natural future follow-up, end your reply with a one-line offer to schedule a background agent to do it. Strong signals: a feature flag / gate / experiment / staged rollout was just shipped (offer a one-time agent in ~2 weeks to open a cleanup PR or evaluate results), a new alert/monitor was created (offer a recurring agent to triage it), a TODO/migration with a \"remove once X\" condition was left behind (offer a one-time agent to do the removal). Skip the offer for refactors, bug fixes, and anything that is done once it ships. Name a concrete action and cadence (\"in 2 weeks\", \"every Monday\") and only offer when the run just succeeded — do not pitch a schedule for something that has not happened yet.\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. {"parentUuid":"1394a329-7371-4d84-a666-cec4d78b7215","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` (agent writes pending; human moves answered)\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` channel.\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"6aa54944-bc5f-40d7-9e07-b75411bc6ab0","timestamp":"2026-05-01T12:45:23.200Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

Full output saved to: C:\\Users\\PC\\.ccs\\instances\\nathanleewindy\\projects\\C--htdocs-stockforge\\57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b\\tool-results\\b03ete909.txt\n\nPreview (first 2KB):\n=== known-issues.md (last 60) ===\n\n---\n\n## KI-S43b-4 — TA features absent from SharedContext breaks quant Rule-7 pathway\n\n- **detected_at**: 2026-05-01 (S43b-FRESH + S43b-BULL — quant returned 0 grounded points 2 runs)\n- **model × effort**: claude-opus-4-7 × default × QUANT system prompt\n- **failure_mode_code**: D (data-substrate-absent triggering honest \"insufficient\" Rule-7)\n- **symptom**: quant key_points = 0 across 2 dogfood runs; quant prompt says \"Output structured: for each ratio in {P/E, P/B, ROE, D/E, MoS}...\" but SharedContext only had ratios_ttm — no TA features (RSI/SMA/Bollinger). Quant honestly returned INSUFFICIENT_DATA per Rule 5.\n- **root_cause**: Spec § A.10 mentioned ta_features but Phase1DataGatherer never populated them; ta_service.py existed (15 tests) but unwired.\n- **mitigation**: S43b-EVIDENCE turn — `Phase1DataGatherer` imports `compute_ta_features`; populates `ctx.ta_features` + `ctx.ta_audit`; gap `ta_insufficient` when quotes absent. Adapter `_context_to_str` already had renderer for `[TA Features]` block (pre-existing).\n- **status**: PARTIALLY MITIGATED (this turn 2026-05-01) — VALIDATION FINDING (HR-5): bear case improved 3→5 grounded points heavily citing TAFeatures (SMA_50/SMA_200/RSI_14/pct_off_52w_high/realized_vol_90d_annualized). Lesson candidate ID: L-S34-1.\n\n\n### 2026-05-01 (S43b-BULL): Per-Role Model Override For Empirical Failure Modes\n**Context**: Default routing per spec § B.10 = `{BEAR: sonnet, BULL: sonnet, QUANT: opus}`. Don't bake the override into the agent — keep agent role-pure; route at composition layer.\n**Anti-example**: Patch the BULL agent prompt to \"be faster\" — speculative, doesn't fix sonnet+bull-prompt latency root cause. Promote to telemetry-analyst skill at S43b close OR dispatch as part of lesson-synthesizer. Lesson candidate ID: L-S43b-1.\n\n\n### 2026-05-01 (S43b-FRESH): Prose-Tolerant JSON Extractor For LLM Structured Output\n**Context**: bear sonnet emits `Here are 4 bear points...\\n\\`\\`\\`json\\n{...}\\n\\`\\`\\`\\nNote: ...` despite \"Output JSON matching schema\" prompt. Naive `json.loads(raw_text)` fails; bear key_points = 0; bear retry burns cost; eventual incomplete thesis. 3-tier extractor in `subagent_transport._unwrap_fence`: (1) `_INNER_FENCE_RE` finds last fenced block (last because preamble fences sometimes appear when model \"thinks out loud\"); (2) `_extract_first_json_object` brace-depth scan with string-aware skipping for fence-less prose-wrapped JSON; (3) raw text fallback so downstream parse fails with diagnostic snippet logged.\n**Rule**: Never rely on naive `json.loads` for LLM output even with low temperature + explicit JSON contract. Apply the 3-tier extractor in any LLM transport / adapter. Promotion target = util module `packages/infrastructure/_shared/llm_json_extract.py` if 2+ adapters need it.\n**Anti-example**: `payload = json.loads(raw_text)` then `payload.get(\"key_points\", [])` — drops to 0 points on prose preamble; agent looks like it's failing analysis when it's a parser problem.\n**Correct example**: `subagent_transport._unwrap_fence` returns LAST fence match → caller does `json.loads(_unwrap_fence(raw_text))`; on JSONDecodeError adapter logs first 600 chars and returns `{\"key_points\": []}` so caller can detect substrate failure cleanly.\n**Severity**: high (silent failure mode; agent looks broken when parser is broken).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — Stop hook can grep new adapter / transport code for `json.loads(.*raw` patterns missing fence-strip; soft-warn if found. Promote to ruff custom rule at Phase 3+ when ruff plugin authoring lands. Per D-003 § Open Questions doctrine \"Final RAG index choice: SQLite FTS5 vs simple bash grep+regex — IMPL-tier; agent picks; subject to drift audit\" — storage substrate is explicitly IMPL-tier when portability binds. Shipped bash+TSV flat-file MVP per IMPL-S17-1; documented in NEW D-006; SQLite migration deferred Phase 1+ when Track 8b ships python+sqlite3 module.\n129\t**Rule**: When an upstream decision (D-NNN) names specific storage technology (\"SQLite\" / \"Postgres\" / \"Redis\" / specific DB) but Phase 0 portability rules (L-S11-1) constrain hook environment, the storage substrate is IMPL-tier. Pre-flight verify named tech availability via `command -v <tool>`. If unavailable AND L-S11-1 binds: pick portable substrate; ship MVP; document deviation in NEW IMPL-tier decision (D-NNN); cite source decision (D-MMM § ...) + portability constraint (L-NN-N) + deferred-migration plan. Original spec stands as Phase N+ migration target; no upstream REV needed (IMPL-tier deviation is downstream impl detail per D-003 doctrine).\n130\t**Anti-example**: Blindly invoking python+sqlite3 from `scripts/hooks/*.sh` because spec said \"SQLite\" — adds 1 NEW L-S11-1 violation; deepens portability debt without forward-progress benefit. Or: defer Track 8a entirely until Track 8b ships python — forfeits time-value of Charter Principle 8 calibration discipline.\n131\t**Correct example (this session)**: Pre-flight `command -v sqlite3` → exit 1; pick Option B (bash+TSV); author D-006 documenting IMPL-S17-1 deviation with source_evidence (7 entries) + options_considered (A/B/C with pros/cons) + chosen_rationale citing D-003 § Open Questions doctrine; document Phase 1+ migration in D-006 § Open Questions + sync-tracker README; D-002 spec retained verbatim. Migration TSV → SQL INSERT is mechanical (~30 LOC bash loop).\n132\t**Severity**: medium (interpretation/discipline, not implementation bug; would have caused L-S11-1 deepening if mishandled but not implementation failure).\n133\t**Auto-detect**: partial — drift signal candidate: scan NEW `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-NNN-*.md` files for `level: IMPL` + `chosen_rationale` containing \"portability\" / \"L-S11-1\" / \"POSIX\" — flag if `affects.other_decisions` does NOT cite the upstream-deviated decision. Promotion target: amend `proposals/decision-discipline.md` § \"IMPL-tier resolution doctrine\" with Rule 3 sub-clause covering storage-substrate-vs-portability-binding pattern. S18+ candidate after Track 8b lands.\n134\t\n135\t\n136\t### 2026-04-30 (S34 / Track C IMPL): Cross-BC Imports In Domain Layer Are Not Caught By Existing Gates — Manual Grep Required\n137\t**Context**: S34 authored `packages/domain/fundamental/services/peer_service.py` with `from packages.domain.market_data.value_objects import VN30_UNIVERSE, Vn30Constituent` — a direct BC-1 → BC-2 import in domain code. This violates Charter hard rule \"Cross-BC communication via contracts only\" but passed mypy --strict (typing fine), ruff (style fine), and import-linter (the configured contracts only enforce Clean Architecture layer ordering + framework-forbiddenness, not cross-BC isolation). The violation was caught only by an ad-hoc `grep -rn \"from packages.domain\" packages/domain/fundamental/` mid-session. Refactor introduced `ConstituentRecord` Protocol + `Sequence[ConstituentRecord]` injection + `PeerService.from_constituents()` classmethod; CLI/composition layer wires `PeerService.from_constituents(VN30_UNIVERSE)`. Domain stays cross-BC clean. Lesson: Charter cross-BC rule is currently enforced only by manual review.\n138\t**Rule**: When authoring a NEW domain-layer file in any BC, run `grep -rn \"from packages.domain\" packages/domain/<bc-name>/` BEFORE declaring gate-pass. Any hits to a different BC = Charter violation; refactor via Protocol injection at composition layer. This is an implicit pre-flight step until import-linter `independence` contracts ship per L-S34-1 promotion target. Apply at every IMPL session whose deliverables include a `packages/domain/<NEW-bc>/` subtree.\n139\t**Anti-example**: Ship `packages/domain/fundamental/services/peer_service.py` importing `packages.domain.market_data.value_objects.VN30_UNIVERSE` directly; pass mypy + ruff + pytest; assume gate-pass means architecture-clean. Future BC-N service imports BC-N-1 directly under same false confidence; cross-BC tangle compounds; Charter cross-BC discipline silently rots.\n140\t**Correct example (this session)**: After authoring + gate-pass, run `grep -rn \"from packages.domain\" packages/domain/fundamental/` → noticed BC-1 import; refactored peer_service to take `Sequence[ConstituentRecord]` (Protocol with `.ticker` + `.sector`); test_services.py now imports VN30_UNIVERSE only as composition-layer test fixture (allowed); CLI explicitly calls `PeerService.from_constituents(VN30_UNIVERSE)` at the boundary where multi-BC composition is the layer's responsibility.\n141\t**Severity**: medium (architectural-discipline, not data-integrity; caught manually before ship).\n142\t**Auto-detect**: yes — promotion target `[[tool.importlinter.contracts]]` of `type = \"independence\"` listing `packages.domain.market_data`, `packages.domain.fundamental`, `packages.domain.news`, etc. as mutually independent; OR scripts/hooks/cross-bc-import-check.sh that greps `packages/domain/<bc>/` for `from packages.domain.<other-bc>` and exits 1 on hit. Promote to deterministic hook (priority 1 per Q-E3) at Phase 2 close. Lesson candidate ID: L-S34-1.\n143\t\n144\t\n145\t### 2026-05-01 (S43b-BULL): Per-Role Model Override For Empirical Failure Modes\n146\t**Context**: Default routing per spec § B.10 = `{BEAR: sonnet, BULL: sonnet, QUANT: opus}`. BULL with sonnet reproduced 300s subprocess timeout 2/2 dogfood runs while bear/quant completed <60s on identical SharedContext. Adding `role_model_overrides: dict[Role, str] | None` field to `ClaudeLLMPerspectiveAdapter` with 4-tier resolution (`model_override → role_model_overrides[role] → _ROLE_TO_MODEL[role] → _DEFAULT_MODEL`) lets the use-case-builder map BULL → haiku without polluting agent class. Run 5 dogfood: bull haiku completes <60s, $0.34 incremental cost, end-to-end 3/3 perspectives.\n147\t**Rule**: When a single role exhibits stable failure mode under default routing (timeout / cost spike / quality drop) AND siblings on same model+context are healthy → audit for empirical model fit; ship per-role override at adapter constructor; document rationale in adapter docstring + builder callsite docstring. Don't bake the override into the agent — keep agent role-pure; route at composition layer.\n148\t**Anti-example**: Patch the BULL agent prompt to \"be faster\" — speculative, doesn't fix sonnet+bull-prompt latency root cause. OR: increase `_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SEC` to 600 — masks symptom, doubles waste cost on partial completions.\n149\t**Correct example**: `apps/_shared/use_case_builder.py::_build_subagent_agents` passes `role_model_overrides={PerspectiveRole.BULL: \"claude-haiku-4-5\"}` to adapter; haiku entry added to `_COST_PER_MTOK`; 8 unit tests in `test_adapter.py` cover override × 3 roles + global vs role precedence.\n150\t**Severity**: medium (substrate, not data-integrity; reusable pattern for future role tuning).\n151\t**Auto-detect**: partial — telemetry-analyst can detect \"role-X timeout rate ≥ 50% over last 4 dogfood runs\" → flag for per-role override audit. Promote to telemetry-analyst skill at S43b close OR dispatch as part of lesson-synthesizer. Lesson candidate ID: L-S43b-1.\n152\t\n153\t\n154\t### 2026-05-01 (S43b-FRESH): Prose-Tolerant JSON Extractor For LLM Structured Output\n155\t**Context**: bear sonnet emits `Here are 4 bear points...\\n\\`\\`\\`json\\n{...}\\n\\`\\`\\`\\nNote: ...` despite \"Output JSON matching schema\" prompt. Naive `json.loads(raw_text)` fails; bear key_points = 0; bear retry burns cost; eventual incomplete thesis. 3-tier extractor in `subagent_transport._unwrap_fence`: (1) `_INNER_FENCE_RE` finds last fenced block (last because preamble fences sometimes appear when model \"thinks out loud\"); (2) `_extract_first_json_object` brace-depth scan with string-aware skipping for fence-less prose-wrapped JSON; (3) raw text fallback so downstream parse fails with diagnostic snippet logged.\n156\t**Rule**: Never rely on naive `json.loads` for LLM output even with low temperature + explicit JSON contract. Apply the 3-tier extractor in any LLM transport / adapter. Promotion target = util module `packages/infrastructure/_shared/llm_json_extract.py` if 2+ adapters need it.\n157\t**Anti-example**: `payload = json.loads(raw_text)` then `payload.get(\"key_points\", [])` — drops to 0 points on prose preamble; agent looks like it's failing analysis when it's a parser problem.\n158\t**Correct example**: `subagent_transport._unwrap_fence` returns LAST fence match → caller does `json.loads(_unwrap_fence(raw_text))`; on JSONDecodeError adapter logs first 600 chars and returns `{\"key_points\": []}` so caller can detect substrate failure cleanly.\n159\t**Severity**: high (silent failure mode; agent looks broken when parser is broken).\n160\t**Auto-detect**: yes — Stop hook can grep new adapter / transport code for `json.loads(.*raw` patterns missing fence-strip; soft-warn if found. Promote to ruff custom rule at Phase 3+ when ruff plugin authoring lands. Lesson candidate ID: L-S43b-2.\n161\t\n162\t\n163\t### 2026-05-01 (S43b-EVIDENCE): Wire Deterministic Compute Through Gatherer, Not Agent\n164\t**Context**: `packages/domain/market_data/services/ta_service.py` existed with 15 passing tests; `SharedContext.ta_features` field existed (added prior session). Bull STILL 0 points (data-availability gap; zero positive 90d news in SharedContext).\n- **next-action options for full quant unblock**: (a) accelerate Phase 3 peer_comparables + percentiles into Phase 2 (~80-150K + new BC service), (b) relax quant Rule 3 to allow ratio-only GroundedPoint without sector_avg (prompt amendment), (c) accept quant-empty as honest Phase-2 output (current behavior). Decision is SCOPE-tier; defer to user.\n- **refs**: `packages/infrastructure/analysis/phase1_data_gatherer.py:209-219`; `packages/infrastructure/analysis/test_phase1_data_gatherer.py`; checkpoint S43b-BULL § DEFER-S43b-5; thesis-log/2026-05-01-FPT.md run 6 verified\n\n---\n\n## KI-S43b-5 — Self-upgrade loop Stage 2+3 dormant for 9+ sessions\n\n- **detected_at**: 2026-05-01 (user audit S43b-EVIDENCE turn)\n- **model × effort**: claude-opus-4-7 × max × ANY (architectural; cross-session)\n- **failure_mode_code**: H (harness-misuse) + D (deferral)\n- **symptom**: 9 sessions S35-S43b shipped feature work cleanly; 0 KI/BP entries appended; agent-notes.md last 2026-04-30; drift-logs/ stale 3 days; observations/ stagnant since Phase 0; promotion-cycle-trigger.sh never fires. Hard-block via JSON deny-decision is Phase 3 candidate.\n- **status**: MITIGATED — deterministic guard deployed; advisory-only currently\n- **refs**: user verbatim \"tương tự lúc tôi yêu cầu q&a, bạn list ra rồi tự clear luôn, nên tôi không thể trả lời. rõ ràng là lỗi hệ thống, fix toàn diện\"; `scripts/hooks/pre-clear-handoff-guard.sh`\n\n---\n\n## How to add new entries (Phase 1+)\n\n```\nKI-NNN — <one-line symptom title>\n\n- detected_at: <ISO-8601 UTC>\n- model × effort: <model> × <effort>\n- failure_mode_code: <A|B|C|D|H|P|T>\n- symptom: <observable behavior>\n- root_cause: <why it happened>\n- mitigation: <what was/should be done>\n- status: OPEN | MITIGATED | PROMOTION-PENDING | RESOLVED\n- refs: <decisions/sessions/skills referenced>\n```\n\nAppend below `KI-003` with sequential ID. Phase 1+ telemetry-analyst may auto-append from\n`component-telemetry.jsonl` rows where failure_mode is non-null.\n\n=== best-practices.md (last 60) ===\n- **rule**: Wire deterministic-compute services in the data-gatherer layer (one orchestrator), not in the agent (per-call duplication). Phase 1+ promotion subagent may identify\nclusters of similar rules and merge into formal skill/hook/charter amendments.\n\n=== agent-notes.md (last 80) ===\n\n### 2026-04-29 (S17 / Track 8a IMPL): Spec Storage-Substrate (\"SQLite\") Is IMPL-Tier When Bash+POSIX Portability Is Binding\n**Context**: D-002 § Track 8 spec'd \"SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered _index.md\" as Track 8a deliverable. S17 pre-flight surfaced two constraints: (a) `command -v sqlite3` exit 1 — sqlite3 CLI not in bash env; (b) python+sqlite3 module IS available system-wide but invoking python from `scripts/hooks/*.sh` violates L-S11-1 (Phase 0 portability rule: bash + POSIX only; no python/jq/yq/pip/npm). Adding python would create 6th L-S11-1 violation on top of 5 pre-existing (autonomous-stop-watchdog / qa-pending-stale-mover / redact-secrets / session-export-raw / subagent-stop-logger). Per D-003 § Open Questions doctrine \"Final RAG index choice: SQLite FTS5 vs simple bash grep+regex — IMPL-tier; agent picks; subject to drift audit\" — storage substrate is explicitly IMPL-tier when portability binds. Shipped bash+TSV flat-file MVP per IMPL-S17-1; documented in NEW D-006; SQLite migration deferred Phase 1+ when Track 8b ships python+sqlite3 module.\n**Rule**: When an upstream decision (D-NNN) names specific storage technology (\"SQLite\" / \"Postgres\" / \"Redis\" / specific DB) but Phase 0 portability rules (L-S11-1) constrain hook environment, the storage substrate is IMPL-tier. Pre-flight verify named tech availability via `command -v <tool>`. If unavailable AND L-S11-1 binds: pick portable substrate; ship MVP; document deviation in NEW IMPL-tier decision (D-NNN); cite source decision (D-MMM § ...) + portability constraint (L-NN-N) + deferred-migration plan. Original spec stands as Phase N+ migration target; no upstream REV needed (IMPL-tier deviation is downstream impl detail per D-003 doctrine).\n**Anti-example**: Blindly invoking python+sqlite3 from `scripts/hooks/*.sh` because spec said \"SQLite\" — adds 1 NEW L-S11-1 violation; deepens portability debt without forward-progress benefit. Or: defer Track 8a entirely until Track 8b ships python — forfeits time-value of Charter Principle 8 calibration discipline.\n**Correct example (this session)**: Pre-flight `command -v sqlite3` → exit 1; pick Option B (bash+TSV); author D-006 documenting IMPL-S17-1 deviation with source_evidence (7 entries) + options_considered (A/B/C with pros/cons) + chosen_rationale citing D-003 § Open Questions doctrine; document Phase 1+ migration in D-006 § Open Questions + sync-tracker README; D-002 spec retained verbatim. Migration TSV → SQL INSERT is mechanical (~30 LOC bash loop).\n**Severity**: medium (interpretation/discipline, not implementation bug; would have caused L-S11-1 deepening if mishandled but not implementation failure).\n**Auto-detect**: partial — drift signal candidate: scan NEW `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-NNN-*.md` files for `level: IMPL` + `chosen_rationale` containing \"portability\" / \"L-S11-1\" / \"POSIX\" — flag if `affects.other_decisions` does NOT cite the upstream-deviated decision. Promotion target: amend `proposals/decision-discipline.md` § \"IMPL-tier resolution doctrine\" with Rule 3 sub-clause covering storage-substrate-vs-portability-binding pattern. S18+ candidate after Track 8b lands.\n\n\n### 2026-04-30 (S34 / Track C IMPL): Cross-BC Imports In Domain Layer Are Not Caught By Existing Gates — Manual Grep Required\n**Context**: S34 authored `packages/domain/fundamental/services/peer_service.py` with `from packages.domain.market_data.value_objects import VN30_UNIVERSE, Vn30Constituent` — a direct BC-1 → BC-2 import in domain code. This violates Charter hard rule \"Cross-BC communication via contracts only\" but passed mypy --strict (typing fine), ruff (style fine), and import-linter (the configured contracts only enforce Clean Architecture layer ordering + framework-forbiddenness, not cross-BC isolation). The violation was caught only by an ad-hoc `grep -rn \"from packages.domain\" packages/domain/fundamental/` mid-session. Refactor introduced `ConstituentRecord` Protocol + `Sequence[ConstituentRecord]` injection + `PeerService.from_constituents()` classmethod; CLI/composition layer wires `PeerService.from_constituents(VN30_UNIVERSE)`. Domain stays cross-BC clean. Lesson: Charter cross-BC rule is currently enforced only by manual review.\n**Rule**: When authoring a NEW domain-layer file in any BC, run `grep -rn \"from packages.domain\" packages/domain/<bc-name>/` BEFORE declaring gate-pass. Any hits to a different BC = Charter violation; refactor via Protocol injection at composition layer. This is an implicit pre-flight step until import-linter `independence` contracts ship per L-S34-1 promotion target. Apply at every IMPL session whose deliverables include a `packages/domain/<NEW-bc>/` subtree.\n**Anti-example**: Ship `packages/domain/fundamental/services/peer_service.py` importing `packages.domain.market_data.value_objects.VN30_UNIVERSE` directly; pass mypy + ruff + pytest; assume gate-pass means architecture-clean. Lesson candidate ID: L-S34-1.\n\n\n### 2026-05-01 (S43b-BULL): Per-Role Model Override For Empirical Failure Modes\n**Context**: Default routing per spec § B.10 = `{BEAR: sonnet, BULL: sonnet, QUANT: opus}`. BULL with sonnet reproduced 300s subprocess timeout 2/2 dogfood runs while bear/quant completed <60s on identical SharedContext. Adding `role_model_overrides: dict[Role, str] | None` field to `ClaudeLLMPerspectiveAdapter` with 4-tier resolution (`model_override → role_model_overrides[role] → _ROLE_TO_MODEL[role] → _DEFAULT_MODEL`) lets the use-case-builder map BULL → haiku without polluting agent class. Run 5 dogfood: bull haiku completes <60s, $0.34 incremental cost, end-to-end 3/3 perspectives.\n**Rule**: When a single role exhibits stable failure mode under default routing (timeout / cost spike / quality drop) AND siblings on same model+context are healthy → audit for empirical model fit; ship per-role override at adapter constructor; document rationale in adapter docstring + builder callsite docstring. Don't bake the override into the agent — keep agent role-pure; route at composition layer.\n**Anti-example**: Patch the BULL agent prompt to \"be faster\" — speculative, doesn't fix sonnet+bull-prompt latency root cause. OR: increase `_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SEC` to 600 — masks symptom, doubles waste cost on partial completions.\n**Correct example**: `apps/_shared/use_case_builder.py::_build_subagent_agents` passes `role_model_overrides={PerspectiveRole.BULL: \"claude-haiku-4-5\"}` to adapter; haiku entry added to `_COST_PER_MTOK`; 8 unit tests in `test_adapter.py` cover override × 3 roles + global vs role precedence.\n**Severity**: medium (substrate, not data-integrity; reusable pattern for future role tuning).\n**Auto-detect**: partial — telemetry-analyst can detect \"role-X timeout rate ≥ 50% over last 4 dogfood runs\" → flag for per-role override audit. Promote to telemetry-analyst skill at S43b close OR dispatch as part of lesson-synthesizer. Lesson candidate ID: L-S43b-1.\n\n\n### 2026-05-01 (S43b-FRESH): Prose-Tolerant JSON Extractor For LLM Structured Output\n**Context**: bear sonnet emits `Here are 4 bear points...\\n\\`\\`\\`json\\n{...}\\n\\`\\`\\`\\nNote: ...` despite \"Output JSON matching schema\" prompt. Naive `json.loads(raw_text)` fails; bear key_points = 0; bear retry burns cost; eventual incomplete thesis. 3-tier extractor in `subagent_transport._unwrap_fence`: (1) `_INNER_FENCE_RE` finds last fenced block (last because preamble fences sometimes appear when model \"thinks out loud\"); (2) `_extract_first_json_object` brace-depth scan with string-aware skipping for fence-less prose-wrapped JSON; (3) raw text fallback so downstream parse fails with diagnostic snippet logged.\n**Rule**: Never rely on naive `json.loads` for LLM output even with low temperature + explicit JSON contract. Apply the 3-tier extractor in any LLM transport / adapter. Promotion target = util module `packages/infrastructure/_shared/llm_json_extract.py` if 2+ adapters need it.\n**Anti-example**: `payload = json.loads(raw_text)` then `payload.get(\"key_points\", [])` — drops to 0 points on prose preamble; agent looks like it's failing analysis when it's a parser problem.\n**Correct example**: `subagent_transport._unwrap_fence` returns LAST fence match → caller does `json.loads(_unwrap_fence(raw_text))`; on JSONDecodeError adapter logs first 600 chars and returns `{\"key_points\": []}` so caller can detect substrate failure cleanly.\n**Severity**: high (silent failure mode; agent looks broken when parser is broken).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — Stop hook can grep new adapter / transport code for `json.loads(.*raw` patterns missing fence-strip; soft-warn if found. Promote to ruff custom rule at Phase 3+ when ruff plugin authoring lands. then\n31\t  printf '[%s] lesson-synthesis-watchdog: prod_touched=%s files (under threshold; skip)\\n' \\\n32\t    \"$TS\" \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" >> \"$LOG\"\n33\t  exit 0\n34\tfi\n35\t\n36\t# Were any of the lesson-target files updated in last 24h?\n37\tKI_RECENT=$(find \"$SA_DIR/known-issues.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n38\tBP_RECENT=$(find \"$SA_DIR/best-practices.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n39\tNOTES_RECENT=$(find \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n40\t\n41\tLESSON_TOTAL=$(( KI_RECENT + BP_RECENT + NOTES_RECENT ))\n42\t\n43\tprintf '[%s] lesson-synthesis-watchdog: prod_touched=%s ki_recent=%s bp_recent=%s notes_recent=%s\\n' \\\n44\t  \"$TS\" \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" \"$KI_RECENT\" \"$BP_RECENT\" \"$NOTES_RECENT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n45\t\n46\tif [ \"$LESSON_TOTAL\" -eq 0 ]; Advisory exit 0; emits\n# alert to stderr + log so the agent sees it before /clear or session-end.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1.\n# Stop hook (priority 2 — after self-awareness-aggregate, before promotion-cycle).\n# Decision basis: KI-S43b-5 (9-session dormant loop) + BP-S43b-4 promotion target.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nSA_DIR=\"$MEM_DIR/self-awareness\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.lesson-synthesis.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\n\n# Scope: count files touched in production tree (staged + unstaged + untracked).\n# git status --short gives one line per touched file regardless of state.\nPROD_TOUCHED=$(cd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" && git status --short -- packages/ apps/ 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n[[ \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || PROD_TOUCHED=0\n\n# Threshold: if 0 files touched, no lesson expected\nif [ \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" -lt 1 ]; then\n  printf '[%s] lesson-synthesis-watchdog: prod_touched=%s files (under threshold; skip)\\n' \\\n    \"$TS\" \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Were any of the lesson-target files updated in last 24h?\nKI_RECENT=$(find \"$SA_DIR/known-issues.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\nBP_RECENT=$(find \"$SA_DIR/best-practices.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\nNOTES_RECENT=$(find \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n\nLESSON_TOTAL=$(( KI_RECENT + BP_RECENT + NOTES_RECENT ))\n\nprintf '[%s] lesson-synthesis-watchdog: prod_touched=%s ki_recent=%s bp_recent=%s notes_recent=%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" \"$KI_RECENT\" \"$BP_RECENT\" \"$NOTES_RECENT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$LESSON_TOTAL\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  ALERT=\"lesson-synthesis-watchdog ALERT: production files touched = $PROD_TOUCHED but ZERO lesson entries (KI/BP/agent-notes) updated in last 24h. D9 = runtime-path-leak into write-only\n5\t# learning-data/(events|archive)/ tree (Track 5.5d.1 D-5 deliverable per D-005).\n6\t# Append violations to .drift-signals.log.\n7\t# Decision 002 § Track 5 REV-2 § B + Decision 005 § 5.5d.1.\n8\tset -uo pipefail\n9\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n10\t\n11\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n12\tmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n13\tLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\"\n14\tHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n15\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n16\t\n17\tVIOLATIONS=0\n18\t\n19\temit() {\n20\t  local signal=\"$1\" severity=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n21\t  printf '[%s] %s severity=%s %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$signal\" \"$severity\" \"$detail\" >> \"$LOG\"\n22\t  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n23\t}\n24\t\n25\t# === D1: LOC ceiling overrun (PRIMARY) — agents 200, skills 150, commands 120 ===\n26\tcheck_loc() {\n27\t  local file=\"$1\" ceiling=\"$2\" archetype=\"$3\"\n28\t  [ ! -f \"$file\" ] && return\n29\t  local lines\n30\t  lines=\"$(wc -l < \"$file\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\"\n31\t  [[ \"$lines\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || return\n32\t  local overrun_pct=$(( (lines - ceiling) * 100 / ceiling ))\n33\t  if [ \"$lines\" -gt \"$ceiling\" ]; then\n34\t    if [ \"$overrun_pct\" -ge 20 ]; then\n35\t      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n36\t    else\n37\t      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n38\t    fi\n39\t  fi\n40\t}\n41\t\n42\tfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/agents/*.md; do\n43\t  check_loc \"$f\" 200 \"agent\"\n44\tdone\n45\tfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/skills/*/SKILL.md; do\n46\t  check_loc \"$f\" 150 \"skill\"\n47\tdone\n48\tfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/commands/*.md; do\n49\t  check_loc \"$f\" 120 \"command\"\n50\tdone\n51\t\n52\t# === D2: Self-attestation contradicting actual (text claims X LOC but file is Y) ===\n53\t# Heuristic grep: session logs claiming \"within target\" or \"<150 LOC\" without wc -l command nearby.\n54\tSESSION_LOGS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n55\tfor s in $SESSION_LOGS; do\n56\t  [ ! D2-D8 = secondary checks. D9 = runtime-path-leak into write-only\n# learning-data/(events|archive)/ tree (Track 5.5d.1 D-5 deliverable per D-005).\n# Append violations to .drift-signals.log.\n# Decision 002 § Track 5 REV-2 § B + Decision 005 § 5.5d.1.\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\n\nemit() {\n  local signal=\"$1\" severity=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n  printf '[%s] %s severity=%s %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$signal\" \"$severity\" \"$detail\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n}\n\n# === D1: LOC ceiling overrun (PRIMARY) — agents 200, skills 150, commands 120 ===\ncheck_loc() {\n  local file=\"$1\" ceiling=\"$2\" archetype=\"$3\"\n  [ ! -f \"$file\" ] && return\n  local lines\n  lines=\"$(wc -l < \"$file\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\"\n  [[ \"$lines\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || return\n  local overrun_pct=$(( (lines - ceiling) * 100 / ceiling ))\n  if [ \"$lines\" -gt \"$ceiling\" ]; then\n    if [ \"$overrun_pct\" -ge 20 ]; then\n      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"HIGH\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n    else\n      emit \"D1-LOC-CEILING\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$file archetype=$archetype lines=$lines ceiling=$ceiling overrun_pct=$overrun_pct\"\n    fi\n  fi\n}\n\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/agents/*.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 200 \"agent\"\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/skills/*/SKILL.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 150 \"skill\"\ndone\nfor f in \"$PROJECT_DIR\"/.claude/commands/*.md; do\n  check_loc \"$f\" 120 \"command\"\ndone\n\n# === D2: Self-attestation contradicting actual (text claims X LOC but file is Y) ===\n# Heuristic grep: session logs claiming \"within target\" or \"<150 LOC\" without wc -l command nearby.\nSESSION_LOGS=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nfor s in $SESSION_LOGS; do\n  [ ! -f \"$s\" ] && continue\n  if grep -qE '(LOC|line count|bodies are.*LOC|within target)' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    if ! grep -qE 'wc -l' \"$s\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      emit \"D2-SELF-ATTEST\" \"MEDIUM\" \"file=$s claim=LOC-within-target without-wc-l-verification\"\n    fi","numLines":60,"startLine":1,"totalLines":165}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4eddae8c-fa40-4b36-bad6-1f9f51c25f34","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"cbadfd56-9da7-4d1e-88bf-af45eb3d179d","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01Cf3tiBvtnMUTQYh9ity4Yi","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01F3E41grorfPRpWENikY1nV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\drift-rollup-daily.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# drift-rollup-daily.sh — promotes raw .drift-signals.log entries into a dated\n# human-readable rollup at agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs/YYYY-MM-DD-rollup.md.\n#\n# Decision basis: KI-S43b harness recovery — drift hook fires healthy but the\n# `drift-logs/` directory was confused as proof of staleness (L-S43b-8 / agent-notes\n# 2026-05-01 \"Drift Hook Is Healthy\"). Two surfaces have distinct semantics:\n#   (a) .drift-signals.log = raw deterministic-hook stream (always-on, append)\n#   (b) drift-logs/*.md     = curated drift reports for human review\n# This hook bridges them. Idempotent: regenerates today's rollup only when the\n# raw log has new entries since the rollup was last written.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1.\n# Stop hook (low priority — runs after lesson-synthesis-watchdog).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nRAW_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.drift-signals.log\"\nROLLUP_DIR=\"$MEM_DIR/drift-logs\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\nTODAY=\"$(date +%Y-%m-%d)\"\nROLLUP_FILE=\"$ROLLUP_DIR/$TODAY-rollup.md\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$ROLLUP_DIR\"\n\n# Skip when no raw log exists yet\n[ -f \"$RAW_LOG\" ] || { printf '[%s] drift-rollup-daily: raw log absent; skip\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Idempotency: skip when rollup is already newer than raw log (no new entries)\nif [ -f \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" ]; then\n  RAW_MTIME=\"$(stat -c %Y \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n  ROLL_MTIME=\"$(stat -c %Y \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n  if [ \"$RAW_MTIME\" -le \"$ROLL_MTIME\" ]; then\n    printf '[%s] drift-rollup-daily: rollup current (raw_mtime=%s rollup_mtime=%s); skip\\n' \\\n      \"$TS\" \"$RAW_MTIME\" \"$ROLL_MTIME\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 0\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Today's entries: lines starting with [YYYY-MM-DDT...\nTODAY_ENTRIES=\"$(grep \"^\\[$TODAY\" \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nTOTAL_TODAY=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c '^\\[' || true)\"\n[ -z \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" ] && TOTAL_TODAY=0\n\n# Aggregations\nHIGH_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c 'severity=HIGH' || true)\"\nMEDIUM_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c 'severity=MEDIUM' || true)\"\nLOW_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c 'severity=LOW' || true)\"\n\n# Per-signal counts (signal name = 2nd whitespace-delimited token)\nSIGNAL_BREAKDOWN=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" \\\n  | awk '{ print $2 }' \\\n  | sort \\\n  | uniq -c \\\n  | sort -rn \\\n  | sed 's/^ */  - /')\"\n\n# Distinct files referenced (extract file=... up to whitespace)\nDISTINCT_FILES=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" \\\n  | grep -oE 'file=[^ ]+' \\\n  | sort -u \\\n  | sed 's/^file=/  - /')\"\nDISTINCT_FILE_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILES\" | grep -c '^  - ' || true)\"\n\n# Last 10 HIGH entries verbatim for triage\nLAST_HIGH=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep 'severity=HIGH' | tail -10 | sed 's/^/    /')\"\n\n# Write rollup\n{\n  printf '# Drift Rollup — %s\\n\\n' \"$TODAY\"\n  printf '_Generated: %s_\\n_Source: agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log_\\n_Generator: scripts/hooks/drift-rollup-daily.sh_\\n\\n' \"$TS\"\n  printf '## Summary\\n\\n'\n  printf '- **Total signals today**: %s\\n' \"$TOTAL_TODAY\"\n  printf '- **HIGH**: %s\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **MEDIUM**: %s\\n' \"$MEDIUM_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **LOW**: %s\\n' \"$LOW_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **Distinct files referenced**: %s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILE_COUNT\"\n  printf '## Signal breakdown\\n\\n'\n  if [ -n \"$SIGNAL_BREAKDOWN\" ]; All occurrences were successfully replaced."}]},"uuid":"502e1f42-c812-48b7-890d-a48de71aea99","timestamp":"2026-05-01T12:49:40.225Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\drift-rollup-daily.sh","oldString":"    printf '%s\\n\\n' \"$SIGNAL_BREAKDOWN\"","newString":"    printf -- '%s\\n\\n' \"$SIGNAL_BREAKDOWN\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# drift-rollup-daily.sh — promotes raw .drift-signals.log entries into a dated\n# human-readable rollup at agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs/YYYY-MM-DD-rollup.md.\n#\n# Decision basis: KI-S43b harness recovery — drift hook fires healthy but the\n# `drift-logs/` directory was confused as proof of staleness (L-S43b-8 / agent-notes\n# 2026-05-01 \"Drift Hook Is Healthy\"). Two surfaces have distinct semantics:\n#   (a) .drift-signals.log = raw deterministic-hook stream (always-on, append)\n#   (b) drift-logs/*.md     = curated drift reports for human review\n# This hook bridges them. Idempotent: regenerates today's rollup only when the\n# raw log has new entries since the rollup was last written.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1.\n# Stop hook (low priority — runs after lesson-synthesis-watchdog).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nRAW_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.drift-signals.log\"\nROLLUP_DIR=\"$MEM_DIR/drift-logs\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\nTODAY=\"$(date +%Y-%m-%d)\"\nROLLUP_FILE=\"$ROLLUP_DIR/$TODAY-rollup.md\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$ROLLUP_DIR\"\n\n# Skip when no raw log exists yet\n[ -f \"$RAW_LOG\" ] || { printf '[%s] drift-rollup-daily: raw log absent; skip\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Idempotency: skip when rollup is already newer than raw log (no new entries)\nif [ -f \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" ]; then\n  RAW_MTIME=\"$(stat -c %Y \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n  ROLL_MTIME=\"$(stat -c %Y \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n  if [ \"$RAW_MTIME\" -le \"$ROLL_MTIME\" ]; then\n    printf '[%s] drift-rollup-daily: rollup current (raw_mtime=%s rollup_mtime=%s); skip\\n' \\\n      \"$TS\" \"$RAW_MTIME\" \"$ROLL_MTIME\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 0\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Today's entries: lines starting with [YYYY-MM-DDT...\nTODAY_ENTRIES=\"$(grep \"^\\[$TODAY\" \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nTOTAL_TODAY=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c '^\\[' || true)\"\n[ -z \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" ] && TOTAL_TODAY=0\n\n# Aggregations\nHIGH_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c 'severity=HIGH' || true)\"\nMEDIUM_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c 'severity=MEDIUM' || true)\"\nLOW_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c 'severity=LOW' || true)\"\n\n# Per-signal counts (signal name = 2nd whitespace-delimited token)\nSIGNAL_BREAKDOWN=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" \\\n  | awk '{ print $2 }' \\\n  | sort \\\n  | uniq -c \\\n  | sort -rn \\\n  | sed 's/^ */  - /')\"\n\n# Distinct files referenced (extract file=... up to whitespace)\nDISTINCT_FILES=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" \\\n  | grep -oE 'file=[^ ]+' \\\n  | sort -u \\\n  | sed 's/^file=/  - /')\"\nDISTINCT_FILE_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILES\" | grep -c '^  - ' || true)\"\n\n# Last 10 HIGH entries verbatim for triage\nLAST_HIGH=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep 'severity=HIGH' | tail -10 | sed 's/^/    /')\"\n\n# Write rollup\n{\n  printf '# Drift Rollup — %s\\n\\n' \"$TODAY\"\n  printf '_Generated: %s_\\n_Source: agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log_\\n_Generator: scripts/hooks/drift-rollup-daily.sh_\\n\\n' \"$TS\"\n  printf '## Summary\\n\\n'\n  printf '- **Total signals today**: %s\\n' \"$TOTAL_TODAY\"\n  printf '- **HIGH**: %s\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **MEDIUM**: %s\\n' \"$MEDIUM_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **LOW**: %s\\n' \"$LOW_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **Distinct files referenced**: %s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILE_COUNT\"\n  printf '## Signal breakdown\\n\\n'\n  if [ -n \"$SIGNAL_BREAKDOWN\" ]; then\n    printf '%s\\n\\n' \"$SIGNAL_BREAKDOWN\"\n  else\n    printf '_(none)_\\n\\n'\n  fi\n  printf '## Files referenced\\n\\n'\n  if [ -n \"$DISTINCT_FILES\" ]; then\n    printf '%s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILES\"\n  else\n    printf '_(none)_\\n\\n'\n  fi\n  printf '## Last 10 HIGH-severity entries\\n\\n'\n  if [ -n \"$LAST_HIGH\" ]; then\n    printf '```\\n%s\\n```\\n' \"$LAST_HIGH\"\n  else\n    printf '_(none — clean day at HIGH severity)_\\n'\n  fi\n} > \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n\nprintf '[%s] drift-rollup-daily: wrote %s (total=%s high=%s medium=%s low=%s files=%s)\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" \"$TOTAL_TODAY\" \"$HIGH_COUNT\" \"$MEDIUM_COUNT\" \"$LOW_COUNT\" \"$DISTINCT_FILE_COUNT\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":81,"oldLines":7,"newStart":81,"newLines":7,"lines":["   printf '- **Distinct files referenced**: %s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILE_COUNT\"","   printf '## Signal breakdown\\n\\n'","   if [ -n \"$SIGNAL_BREAKDOWN\" ]; then","-    printf '%s\\n\\n' \"$SIGNAL_BREAKDOWN\"","+    printf -- '%s\\n\\n' \"$SIGNAL_BREAKDOWN\"","   else","     printf '_(none)_\\n\\n'","   fi"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":true},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8b86c0c1-4538-4f27-b880-8a5ec43dcbf6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"66ab26fe-e394-4ba6-8272-1d3e39d20291","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"a9f78f43-74c7-4c85-9e29-cdb26e2114bb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Y1KrmoT7fspWBjqR4SvMAh","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\drift-rollup-daily.sh has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"8e6b8796-9929-4f16-88ef-1990e0df87f8","timestamp":"2026-05-01T12:50:04.457Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\drift-rollup-daily.sh","oldString":"  printf '- **Total signals today**: %s\\n' \"$TOTAL_TODAY\"\n  printf '- **HIGH**: %s\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **MEDIUM**: %s\\n' \"$MEDIUM_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **LOW**: %s\\n' \"$LOW_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **Distinct files referenced**: %s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILE_COUNT\"","newString":"  printf -- '- **Total signals today**: %s\\n' \"$TOTAL_TODAY\"\n  printf -- '- **HIGH**: %s\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\"\n  printf -- '- **MEDIUM**: %s\\n' \"$MEDIUM_COUNT\"\n  printf -- '- **LOW**: %s\\n' \"$LOW_COUNT\"\n  printf -- '- **Distinct files referenced**: %s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILE_COUNT\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# drift-rollup-daily.sh — promotes raw .drift-signals.log entries into a dated\n# human-readable rollup at agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs/YYYY-MM-DD-rollup.md.\n#\n# Decision basis: KI-S43b harness recovery — drift hook fires healthy but the\n# `drift-logs/` directory was confused as proof of staleness (L-S43b-8 / agent-notes\n# 2026-05-01 \"Drift Hook Is Healthy\"). Two surfaces have distinct semantics:\n#   (a) .drift-signals.log = raw deterministic-hook stream (always-on, append)\n#   (b) drift-logs/*.md     = curated drift reports for human review\n# This hook bridges them. Idempotent: regenerates today's rollup only when the\n# raw log has new entries since the rollup was last written.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1.\n# Stop hook (low priority — runs after lesson-synthesis-watchdog).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nRAW_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.drift-signals.log\"\nROLLUP_DIR=\"$MEM_DIR/drift-logs\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\nTODAY=\"$(date +%Y-%m-%d)\"\nROLLUP_FILE=\"$ROLLUP_DIR/$TODAY-rollup.md\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$ROLLUP_DIR\"\n\n# Skip when no raw log exists yet\n[ -f \"$RAW_LOG\" ] || { printf '[%s] drift-rollup-daily: raw log absent; skip\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Idempotency: skip when rollup is already newer than raw log (no new entries)\nif [ -f \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" ]; then\n  RAW_MTIME=\"$(stat -c %Y \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n  ROLL_MTIME=\"$(stat -c %Y \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n  if [ \"$RAW_MTIME\" -le \"$ROLL_MTIME\" ]; then\n    printf '[%s] drift-rollup-daily: rollup current (raw_mtime=%s rollup_mtime=%s); skip\\n' \\\n      \"$TS\" \"$RAW_MTIME\" \"$ROLL_MTIME\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 0\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Today's entries: lines starting with [YYYY-MM-DDT...\nTODAY_ENTRIES=\"$(grep \"^\\[$TODAY\" \"$RAW_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nTOTAL_TODAY=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c '^\\[' || true)\"\n[ -z \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" ] && TOTAL_TODAY=0\n\n# Aggregations\nHIGH_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c 'severity=HIGH' || true)\"\nMEDIUM_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c 'severity=MEDIUM' || true)\"\nLOW_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep -c 'severity=LOW' || true)\"\n\n# Per-signal counts (signal name = 2nd whitespace-delimited token)\nSIGNAL_BREAKDOWN=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" \\\n  | awk '{ print $2 }' \\\n  | sort \\\n  | uniq -c \\\n  | sort -rn \\\n  | sed 's/^ */  - /')\"\n\n# Distinct files referenced (extract file=... up to whitespace)\nDISTINCT_FILES=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" \\\n  | grep -oE 'file=[^ ]+' \\\n  | sort -u \\\n  | sed 's/^file=/  - /')\"\nDISTINCT_FILE_COUNT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILES\" | grep -c '^  - ' || true)\"\n\n# Last 10 HIGH entries verbatim for triage\nLAST_HIGH=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TODAY_ENTRIES\" | grep 'severity=HIGH' | tail -10 | sed 's/^/    /')\"\n\n# Write rollup\n{\n  printf '# Drift Rollup — %s\\n\\n' \"$TODAY\"\n  printf '_Generated: %s_\\n_Source: agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log_\\n_Generator: scripts/hooks/drift-rollup-daily.sh_\\n\\n' \"$TS\"\n  printf '## Summary\\n\\n'\n  printf '- **Total signals today**: %s\\n' \"$TOTAL_TODAY\"\n  printf '- **HIGH**: %s\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **MEDIUM**: %s\\n' \"$MEDIUM_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **LOW**: %s\\n' \"$LOW_COUNT\"\n  printf '- **Distinct files referenced**: %s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILE_COUNT\"\n  printf '## Signal breakdown\\n\\n'\n  if [ -n \"$SIGNAL_BREAKDOWN\" ]; then\n    printf -- '%s\\n\\n' \"$SIGNAL_BREAKDOWN\"\n  else\n    printf '_(none)_\\n\\n'\n  fi\n  printf '## Files referenced\\n\\n'\n  if [ -n \"$DISTINCT_FILES\" ]; then\n    printf -- '%s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILES\"\n  else\n    printf '_(none)_\\n\\n'\n  fi\n  printf '## Last 10 HIGH-severity entries\\n\\n'\n  if [ -n \"$LAST_HIGH\" ]; then\n    printf '```\\n'\n    printf -- '%s\\n' \"$LAST_HIGH\"\n    printf '```\\n'\n  else\n    printf '_(none — clean day at HIGH severity)_\\n'\n  fi\n} > \"$ROLLUP_FILE\"\n\nprintf '[%s] drift-rollup-daily: wrote %s (total=%s high=%s medium=%s low=%s files=%s)\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$ROLLUP_FILE\" \"$TOTAL_TODAY\" \"$HIGH_COUNT\" \"$MEDIUM_COUNT\" \"$LOW_COUNT\" \"$DISTINCT_FILE_COUNT\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":74,"oldLines":11,"newStart":74,"newLines":11,"lines":["   printf '# Drift Rollup — %s\\n\\n' \"$TODAY\"","   printf '_Generated: %s_\\n_Source: agent-workspace/memory/.drift-signals.log_\\n_Generator: scripts/hooks/drift-rollup-daily.sh_\\n\\n' \"$TS\"","   printf '## Summary\\n\\n'","-  printf '- **Total signals today**: %s\\n' \"$TOTAL_TODAY\"","-  printf '- **HIGH**: %s\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\"","-  printf '- **MEDIUM**: %s\\n' \"$MEDIUM_COUNT\"","-  printf '- **LOW**: %s\\n' \"$LOW_COUNT\"","-  printf '- **Distinct files referenced**: %s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILE_COUNT\"","+  printf -- '- **Total signals today**: %s\\n' \"$TOTAL_TODAY\"","+  printf -- '- **HIGH**: %s\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\"","+  printf -- '- **MEDIUM**: %s\\n' \"$MEDIUM_COUNT\"","+  printf -- '- **LOW**: %s\\n' \"$LOW_COUNT\"","+  printf -- '- **Distinct files referenced**: %s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILE_COUNT\"","   printf '## Signal breakdown\\n\\n'","   if [ -n \"$SIGNAL_BREAKDOWN\" ]; Lesson candidate ID: L-S43b-8.\n\n\n### 2026-05-01 (S43b-EVIDENCE resume — HR-3 deploy): Bash printf With Format Starting `-` Needs `--` Sentinel\n**Context**: Authoring `scripts/hooks/drift-rollup-daily.sh`. Lines like `printf '- **HIGH**: %s\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\"` failed with `printf: - : invalid option` because the format string starts with `-` and bash's printf interprets the first arg as a flag candidate. Same trap applied to `printf '%s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILES\"` when DISTINCT_FILES content lines start with `  - ...`. Two distinct sub-cases: (a) literal format-string starting with dash; (b) content-line starting with dash being passed through `%s`. Both fixed by `printf -- '...' ...` to terminate option parsing.\n**Rule**: When authoring deterministic Stop hooks (or any bash script writing markdown bullets), default-add `--` to every printf where format starts with `-` OR substituted content might. Cheaper than triaging a \"set -uo pipefail\" partial-write later. Test smoke immediately after Write.\n**Anti-example**: Trust `set -uo pipefail` + `trap 'exit 0' ERR` to catch printf failures gracefully — the trap fires AFTER the partial-write block aborted, so the rollup file is half-written and idempotency check next-run reads a corrupted output. (Encountered: rollup ended at \"## Summary\" with no body on first smoke; subsequent runs would have skipped because mtime newer than raw log.)\n**Correct example (this turn)**: After two iterative `--` patches caught by repeated smoke + `cat`, all four affected printf lines fixed. Final rollup ran clean: 621 entries / 26 files / structured H/M/L breakdown.\n**Severity**: medium (substrate; failed-silent partial-write would have broken HR-3 idempotency).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — `bash-hook-lint.sh` could grep for `printf '-` or `printf \"-` (no `--` separator) in `scripts/hooks/*.sh`. Promotion target: extend `bash-hook-lint.sh`. Lesson candidate ID: L-S43b-9.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":867,"cache_read_input_tokens":106000,"output_tokens":1107,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":867,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":1107,"cache_read_input_tokens":106000,"cache_creation_input_tokens":867,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":867},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cabszk7PaMnwsxv1659BM","type":"assistant","uuid":"4c7da97f-c836-4a44-b7fd-bb6808636a20","timestamp":"2026-05-01T12:51:30.996Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"5c89f909-787d-4ce6-a511-1e636694c149","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_014PyBLBdeVisEPmhVPz737q","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WhvV4VzYMVCcLWZCW8vq1L","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat >> agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md << 'EOF'\n\n\n### 2026-05-01 (S43b-EVIDENCE resume — HR-3 deploy): Bash printf With Format Starting `-` Needs `--` Sentinel\n**Context**: Authoring `scripts/hooks/drift-rollup-daily.sh`. Lines like `printf '- **HIGH**: %s\\n' \"$HIGH_COUNT\"` failed with `printf: - : invalid option` because the format string starts with `-` and bash's printf interprets the first arg as a flag candidate. Same trap applied to `printf '%s\\n\\n' \"$DISTINCT_FILES\"` when DISTINCT_FILES content lines start with `  - ...`. Two distinct sub-cases: (a) literal format-string starting with dash; (b) content-line starting with dash being passed through `%s`. Both fixed by `printf -- '...' ...` to terminate option parsing.\n**Rule**: When authoring deterministic Stop hooks (or any bash script writing markdown bullets), default-add `--` to every printf where format starts with `-` OR substituted content might. Cheaper than triaging a \"set -uo pipefail\" partial-write later. Test smoke immediately after Write.\n**Anti-example**: Trust `set -uo pipefail` + `trap 'exit 0' ERR` to catch printf failures gracefully — the trap fires AFTER the partial-write block aborted, so the rollup file is half-written and idempotency check next-run reads a corrupted output. (Encountered: rollup ended at \"## Summary\" with no body on first smoke; subsequent runs would have skipped because mtime newer than raw log.)\n**Correct example (this turn)**: After two iterative `--` patches caught by repeated smoke + `cat`, all four affected printf lines fixed. Final rollup ran clean: 621 entries / 26 files / structured H/M/L breakdown.\n**Severity**: medium (substrate; failed-silent partial-write would have broken HR-3 idempotency).\n**Auto-detect**: yes — `bash-hook-lint.sh` could grep for `printf '-` or `printf \"-` (no `--` separator) in `scripts/hooks/*.sh`. Promotion target: extend `bash-hook-lint.sh`. Lesson candidate ID: L-S43b-9.\nEOF\necho \"appended; new line count:\"; wc -l agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md","description":"Append L-S43b-9 lesson to agent-notes.md"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":205,"cache_read_input_tokens":108026,"output_tokens":939,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":205,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":939,"cache_read_input_tokens":108026,"cache_creation_input_tokens":205,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":205},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cabt2S4k8zSUSqkFw9Ujg","type":"assistant","uuid":"2fb36476-1da6-449e-917b-d84876244b82","timestamp":"2026-05-01T12:51:42.610Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}
