{"type":"last-prompt","lastPrompt":"sao lại dừng lại rồi?","leafUuid":"19cc19a5-2e9a-4ac5-9bba-b6e69127e123","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b"}
{"parentUuid":"19cc19a5-2e9a-4ac5-9bba-b6e69127e123","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"f8d66ccb-5ad5-4b7b-b373-f98fda361da8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EWDxTkjxL15FhS1RRpvHoM","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env bash\n2\t# pre-clear-handoff-guard.sh — durable handoff invariant (BP-S43b-6 / KI-S43b-6).\n3\t#\n4\t# At Stop hook (priority 1 — before aggregator), if the LATEST session log shows\n5\t# pending-decision triggers (AskUserQuestion / numbered options menu / SCOPE-tier\n6\t# deferral / wind-down threshold), AND checkpoints/latest.md is older than the\n7\t# session start, emit ALERT — the agent should write a checkpoint before /clear.\n8\t#\n9\t# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1. Advisory exit 0.\n10\t# User-reported failure mode: agent listed Q&A options, /clear fired, options lost.\n11\t#\n12\t# Heuristic phrases (Vietnamese + English) — append-only growth:\n13\t#   \"AskUserQuestion\" — agent dispatched question tool\n14\t#   \"(a)\" \"(b)\" \"(c)\" \"(d)\" — lettered option menu\n15\t#   \"SCOPE-tier\" \"charter-tier\" — deferral surface\n16\t#   \"wind_down\" \"cliff\" — budget threshold breached\n17\t#   \"Q&A\" \"q-and-a\" — pending bundle reference\n18\t\n19\tset -uo pipefail\n20\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n21\t\n22\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n23\tMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n24\tLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.handoff-guard.log\"\n25\tHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n26\tCHECKPOINT=\"$MEM_DIR/checkpoints/latest.md\"\n27\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n28\t\n29\tmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\n30\t\n31\t# Find the most-recently modified session log.\n32\tLATEST_SESSION=$(find \"$MEM_DIR/sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)\n33\t\n34\t# Also check transcript via raw-sessions if available.\n35\tLATEST_RAW=$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/raw-sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)\n36\t\n37\tPENDING=0\n38\tTRIGGER_LIST=\"\"\n39\t\n40\tscan_for_triggers() {\n41\t  local file=\"$1\"\n42\t  [ ! -f \"$file\" ] && return\n43\t  if grep -qiE 'AskUserQuestion|^\\s*\\(a\\)|^\\s*\\(b\\)|^\\s*\\(c\\)|SCOPE-tier|charter-tier|wind_down|cliff fired|q-and-a/pending' \"$file\" 2>/dev/null; then\n44\t    PENDING=1\n45\t    TRIGGER_LIST=\"$TRIGGER_LIST $(basename \"$file\")\"\n46\t  fi\n47\t}\n48\t\n49\tscan_for_triggers \"$LATEST_SESSION\"\n50\tscan_for_triggers \"$LATEST_RAW\"\n51\t\n52\t# Compare checkpoint mtime vs session start (heuristic: 2h ago).\n53\tCHECKPOINT_FRESH=0\n54\tif [ -f \"$CHECKPOINT\" ]; then\n55\t  if find \"$CHECKPOINT\" -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then\n56\t    CHECKPOINT_FRESH=1\n57\t  fi\n58\tfi\n59\t\n60\tprintf '[%s] pre-clear-handoff-guard: pending=%s checkpoint_fresh=%s triggers=%s\\n' \\\n61\t  \"$TS\" \"$PENDING\" \"$CHECKPOINT_FRESH\" \"${TRIGGER_LIST:-none}\" >> \"$LOG\"\n62\t\n63\tif [ \"$PENDING\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$CHECKPOINT_FRESH\" -eq 0 ]; then\n64\t  ALERT=\"pre-clear-handoff-guard ALERT: pending-decision triggers detected (${TRIGGER_LIST}) but checkpoints/latest.md is stale (>2h). Write checkpoint before /clear per BP-S43b-6.\"\n65\t  printf '[%s] %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALERT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n66\t  printf '[%s] pre-clear-handoff-guard: ALERT pending=1 stale_checkpoint=1\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n67\t  echo \"$ALERT\" >&2\n68\tfi\n69\t\n70\texit 0\n71\t"}]},"uuid":"2adffb5c-66de-4c41-ace8-b7f1f99a6877","timestamp":"2026-05-01T14:09:57.916Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\pre-clear-handoff-guard.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# pre-clear-handoff-guard.sh — durable handoff invariant (BP-S43b-6 / KI-S43b-6).\n#\n# At Stop hook (priority 1 — before aggregator), if the LATEST session log shows\n# pending-decision triggers (AskUserQuestion / numbered options menu / SCOPE-tier\n# deferral / wind-down threshold), AND checkpoints/latest.md is older than the\n# session start, emit ALERT — the agent should write a checkpoint before /clear.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1. Advisory exit 0.\n# User-reported failure mode: agent listed Q&A options, /clear fired, options lost.\n#\n# Heuristic phrases (Vietnamese + English) — append-only growth:\n#   \"AskUserQuestion\" — agent dispatched question tool\n#   \"(a)\" \"(b)\" \"(c)\" \"(d)\" — lettered option menu\n#   \"SCOPE-tier\" \"charter-tier\" — deferral surface\n#   \"wind_down\" \"cliff\" — budget threshold breached\n#   \"Q&A\" \"q-and-a\" — pending bundle reference\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.handoff-guard.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nCHECKPOINT=\"$MEM_DIR/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\n\n# Find the most-recently modified session log.\nLATEST_SESSION=$(find \"$MEM_DIR/sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)\n\n# Also check transcript via raw-sessions if available.\nLATEST_RAW=$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/raw-sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)\n\nPENDING=0\nTRIGGER_LIST=\"\"\n\nscan_for_triggers() {\n  local file=\"$1\"\n  [ ! -f \"$file\" ] && return\n  if grep -qiE 'AskUserQuestion|^\\s*\\(a\\)|^\\s*\\(b\\)|^\\s*\\(c\\)|SCOPE-tier|charter-tier|wind_down|cliff fired|q-and-a/pending' \"$file\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    PENDING=1\n    TRIGGER_LIST=\"$TRIGGER_LIST $(basename \"$file\")\"\n  fi\n}\n\nscan_for_triggers \"$LATEST_SESSION\"\nscan_for_triggers \"$LATEST_RAW\"\n\n# Compare checkpoint mtime vs session start (heuristic: 2h ago).\nCHECKPOINT_FRESH=0\nif [ -f \"$CHECKPOINT\" ]; then\n  if find \"$CHECKPOINT\" -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then\n    CHECKPOINT_FRESH=1\n  fi\nfi\n\nprintf '[%s] pre-clear-handoff-guard: pending=%s checkpoint_fresh=%s triggers=%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$PENDING\" \"$CHECKPOINT_FRESH\" \"${TRIGGER_LIST:-none}\" >> \"$LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$PENDING\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$CHECKPOINT_FRESH\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  ALERT=\"pre-clear-handoff-guard ALERT: pending-decision triggers detected (${TRIGGER_LIST}) but checkpoints/latest.md is stale (>2h). Write checkpoint before /clear per BP-S43b-6.\"\n  printf '[%s] %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALERT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  printf '[%s] pre-clear-handoff-guard: ALERT pending=1 stale_checkpoint=1\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  echo \"$ALERT\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","numLines":71,"startLine":1,"totalLines":71}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"19cc19a5-2e9a-4ac5-9bba-b6e69127e123","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

Opt in via either:\n#                STOCKFORGE_HANDOFF_HARDBLOCK=1   (per-session env)\n#                STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE=strict   (project-wide profile)\n#              When agent then writes a checkpoint, next Stop sees fresh mtime\n#              and auto-clears the block (loop is finite, ≤1 hard-block per\n#              triggering pattern).\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1.\n# User-reported failure mode: agent listed Q&A options, /clear fired, options lost.\n#\n# Heuristic phrases (Vietnamese + English) — append-only growth:\n#   \"AskUserQuestion\" — agent dispatched question tool\n#   \"(a)\" \"(b)\" \"(c)\" \"(d)\" — lettered option menu\n#   \"SCOPE-tier\" \"charter-tier\" — deferral surface\n#   \"wind_down\" \"cliff\" — budget threshold breached\n#   \"Q&A\" \"q-and-a\" — pending bundle reference","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# pre-clear-handoff-guard.sh — durable handoff invariant (BP-S43b-6 / KI-S43b-6).\n#\n# At Stop hook (priority 1 — before aggregator), if the LATEST session log shows\n# pending-decision triggers (AskUserQuestion / numbered options menu / SCOPE-tier\n# deferral / wind-down threshold), AND checkpoints/latest.md is older than the\n# session start, emit ALERT — the agent should write a checkpoint before /clear.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1. Advisory exit 0.\n# User-reported failure mode: agent listed Q&A options, /clear fired, options lost.\n#\n# Heuristic phrases (Vietnamese + English) — append-only growth:\n#   \"AskUserQuestion\" — agent dispatched question tool\n#   \"(a)\" \"(b)\" \"(c)\" \"(d)\" — lettered option menu\n#   \"SCOPE-tier\" \"charter-tier\" — deferral surface\n#   \"wind_down\" \"cliff\" — budget threshold breached\n#   \"Q&A\" \"q-and-a\" — pending bundle reference\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.handoff-guard.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nCHECKPOINT=\"$MEM_DIR/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\n\n# Find the most-recently modified session log.\nLATEST_SESSION=$(find \"$MEM_DIR/sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)\n\n# Also check transcript via raw-sessions if available.\nLATEST_RAW=$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/raw-sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)\n\nPENDING=0\nTRIGGER_LIST=\"\"\n\nscan_for_triggers() {\n  local file=\"$1\"\n  [ ! -f \"$file\" ] && return\n  if grep -qiE 'AskUserQuestion|^\\s*\\(a\\)|^\\s*\\(b\\)|^\\s*\\(c\\)|SCOPE-tier|charter-tier|wind_down|cliff fired|q-and-a/pending' \"$file\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    PENDING=1\n    TRIGGER_LIST=\"$TRIGGER_LIST $(basename \"$file\")\"\n  fi\n}\n\nscan_for_triggers \"$LATEST_SESSION\"\nscan_for_triggers \"$LATEST_RAW\"\n\n# Compare checkpoint mtime vs session start (heuristic: 2h ago).\nCHECKPOINT_FRESH=0\nif [ -f \"$CHECKPOINT\" ]; then\n  if find \"$CHECKPOINT\" -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then\n    CHECKPOINT_FRESH=1\n  fi\nfi\n\nprintf '[%s] pre-clear-handoff-guard: pending=%s checkpoint_fresh=%s triggers=%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$PENDING\" \"$CHECKPOINT_FRESH\" \"${TRIGGER_LIST:-none}\" >> \"$LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$PENDING\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$CHECKPOINT_FRESH\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  ALERT=\"pre-clear-handoff-guard ALERT: pending-decision triggers detected (${TRIGGER_LIST}) but checkpoints/latest.md is stale (>2h). Write checkpoint before /clear per BP-S43b-6.\"\n  printf '[%s] %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALERT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  printf '[%s] pre-clear-handoff-guard: ALERT pending=1 stale_checkpoint=1\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  echo \"$ALERT\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":4,"oldLines":9,"newStart":4,"newLines":19,"lines":[" # At Stop hook (priority 1 — before aggregator), if the LATEST session log shows"," # pending-decision triggers (AskUserQuestion / numbered options menu / SCOPE-tier"," # deferral / wind-down threshold), AND checkpoints/latest.md is older than the","-# session start, emit ALERT — the agent should write a checkpoint before /clear.","+# session start, surface alert."," #","-# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1. Advisory exit 0.","+# Modes (HR-7 — hard-block opt-in):","+#   default  → advisory: stderr echo + log; exit 0 (user/agent may miss it)","+#   strict   → hard-signal: exit 2 (Claude Code surfaces stderr to assistant","+#              transcript, forcing acknowledgement). Opt in via either:","+#                STOCKFORGE_HANDOFF_HARDBLOCK=1   (per-session env)","+#                STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE=strict   (project-wide profile)","+#              When agent then writes a checkpoint, next Stop sees fresh mtime","+#              and auto-clears the block (loop is finite, ≤1 hard-block per","+#              triggering pattern).","+#","+# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1."," # User-reported failure mode: agent listed Q&A options, /clear fired, options lost."," #"," # Heuristic phrases (Vietnamese + English) — append-only growth:"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c4151464-38be-446a-8a2e-38be0f03b757","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

Default advisory exit 0.\n  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_HANDOFF_HARDBLOCK:-0}\" = \"1\" ] || [ \"${STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE:-standard}\" = \"strict\" ]; then\n    printf '[%s] pre-clear-handoff-guard: HARD-BLOCK exit=2 (strict profile)\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit 0","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# pre-clear-handoff-guard.sh — durable handoff invariant (BP-S43b-6 / KI-S43b-6).\n#\n# At Stop hook (priority 1 — before aggregator), if the LATEST session log shows\n# pending-decision triggers (AskUserQuestion / numbered options menu / SCOPE-tier\n# deferral / wind-down threshold), AND checkpoints/latest.md is older than the\n# session start, surface alert.\n#\n# Modes (HR-7 — hard-block opt-in):\n#   default  → advisory: stderr echo + log; exit 0 (user/agent may miss it)\n#   strict   → hard-signal: exit 2 (Claude Code surfaces stderr to assistant\n#              transcript, forcing acknowledgement). Opt in via either:\n#                STOCKFORGE_HANDOFF_HARDBLOCK=1   (per-session env)\n#                STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE=strict   (project-wide profile)\n#              When agent then writes a checkpoint, next Stop sees fresh mtime\n#              and auto-clears the block (loop is finite, ≤1 hard-block per\n#              triggering pattern).\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1.\n# User-reported failure mode: agent listed Q&A options, /clear fired, options lost.\n#\n# Heuristic phrases (Vietnamese + English) — append-only growth:\n#   \"AskUserQuestion\" — agent dispatched question tool\n#   \"(a)\" \"(b)\" \"(c)\" \"(d)\" — lettered option menu\n#   \"SCOPE-tier\" \"charter-tier\" — deferral surface\n#   \"wind_down\" \"cliff\" — budget threshold breached\n#   \"Q&A\" \"q-and-a\" — pending bundle reference\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.handoff-guard.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nCHECKPOINT=\"$MEM_DIR/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\n\n# Find the most-recently modified session log.\nLATEST_SESSION=$(find \"$MEM_DIR/sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)\n\n# Also check transcript via raw-sessions if available.\nLATEST_RAW=$(find \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/raw-sessions\" -name '*.md' -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | head -1 || true)\n\nPENDING=0\nTRIGGER_LIST=\"\"\n\nscan_for_triggers() {\n  local file=\"$1\"\n  [ ! -f \"$file\" ] && return\n  if grep -qiE 'AskUserQuestion|^\\s*\\(a\\)|^\\s*\\(b\\)|^\\s*\\(c\\)|SCOPE-tier|charter-tier|wind_down|cliff fired|q-and-a/pending' \"$file\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    PENDING=1\n    TRIGGER_LIST=\"$TRIGGER_LIST $(basename \"$file\")\"\n  fi\n}\n\nscan_for_triggers \"$LATEST_SESSION\"\nscan_for_triggers \"$LATEST_RAW\"\n\n# Compare checkpoint mtime vs session start (heuristic: 2h ago).\nCHECKPOINT_FRESH=0\nif [ -f \"$CHECKPOINT\" ]; then\n  if find \"$CHECKPOINT\" -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then\n    CHECKPOINT_FRESH=1\n  fi\nfi\n\nprintf '[%s] pre-clear-handoff-guard: pending=%s checkpoint_fresh=%s triggers=%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$PENDING\" \"$CHECKPOINT_FRESH\" \"${TRIGGER_LIST:-none}\" >> \"$LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$PENDING\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$CHECKPOINT_FRESH\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  ALERT=\"pre-clear-handoff-guard ALERT: pending-decision triggers detected (${TRIGGER_LIST}) but checkpoints/latest.md is stale (>2h). Write checkpoint before /clear per BP-S43b-6.\"\n  printf '[%s] %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALERT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  printf '[%s] pre-clear-handoff-guard: ALERT pending=1 stale_checkpoint=1\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  echo \"$ALERT\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":75,"oldLines":6,"newStart":75,"newLines":13,"lines":["   printf '[%s] %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALERT\" >> \"$LOG\"","   printf '[%s] pre-clear-handoff-guard: ALERT pending=1 stale_checkpoint=1\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"","   echo \"$ALERT\" >&2","+","+  # HR-7: opt-in hard-signal via exit 2 (Claude Code surfaces stderr to","+  # assistant transcript, forcing acknowledgement). Default advisory exit 0.","+  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_HANDOFF_HARDBLOCK:-0}\" = \"1\" ] || [ \"${STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE:-standard}\" = \"strict\" ]; then","+    printf '[%s] pre-clear-handoff-guard: HARD-BLOCK exit=2 (strict profile)\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"","+    exit 2","+  fi"," fi"," "," exit 0"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fc1c1dea-8394-47da-b1e2-c5dbf472523e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"2245e61c-5d82-46a4-9d78-01828de00477","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_019TXBgdzzTASW9PGEzxtdwF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Pmbf8Ubmsn4kfC71F4x5mA","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\harness-recovery-dod-watchdog.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# harness-recovery-dod-watchdog.sh — prevents premature-stop during charter-tier\n# recovery arcs (KI-S43b-7 / BP-S43b-7 / L-S43b-10).\n#\n# Mechanism:\n#   1. Scan agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md for the ACTIVE recovery\n#      row (heuristic: lines containing \"HARNESS-RECOVERY\" or \"fix toàn diện\" or\n#      \"HR-[0-9]\" markers).\n#   2. If ≥1 line within recovery row contains \"⏳ PENDING\" (the convention from\n#      BP-S43b-7), recovery DoD is NOT MET.\n#   3. ALERT to stderr in default mode; exit 2 in strict mode (agent must keep\n#      working, not write tidy summary).\n#\n# Modes:\n#   default  → stderr ALERT + log; exit 0 (advisory)\n#   strict   → exit 2 when DoD-not-met (Claude Code surfaces stderr to assistant)\n#              Opt in via STOCKFORGE_DOD_HARDBLOCK=1 OR STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE=strict.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1.\n# Stop hook (after lesson-synthesis-watchdog, before promotion-cycle-trigger).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nEXEC_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.dod-watchdog.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\n\n[ -f \"$EXEC_FILE\" ] || { printf '[%s] dod-watchdog: current-execution.md absent; skip\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Count ⏳ PENDING markers within HR-* lines.\nPENDING_COUNT=\"$(grep -cE 'HR-[0-9]+.*⏳ PENDING' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n[[ \"$PENDING_COUNT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || PENDING_COUNT=0\n\n# Also surface count of explicit ⏳ PENDING lines (broader catch, not just HR-*).\nALL_PENDING=\"$(grep -cE '⏳ PENDING' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n[[ \"$ALL_PENDING\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || ALL_PENDING=0\n\n# List the actual pending lines for the alert + log.\nPENDING_LINES=\"$(grep -nE '⏳ PENDING' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null | head -10 || true)\"\n\nprintf '[%s] dod-watchdog: hr_pending=%s all_pending=%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$PENDING_COUNT\" \"$ALL_PENDING\" >> \"$LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$ALL_PENDING\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  ALERT=\"harness-recovery-dod-watchdog ALERT: ${ALL_PENDING} ⏳ PENDING items remain in current-execution.md (HR-* count=${PENDING_COUNT}). Per BP-S43b-7, agent must not stop with PENDING items. Continue executing or mark items 🔒 GATED-HUMAN / 🔭 PHASE-N-DEFERRED with explicit reason.\"\n  printf '[%s] %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALERT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  printf '[%s] dod-watchdog: ALERT pending=%s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALL_PENDING\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  echo \"$ALERT\" >&2\n  if [ -n \"$PENDING_LINES\" ]; then\n    echo \"Pending lines (first 10):\" >&2\n    printf -- '%s\\n' \"$PENDING_LINES\" >&2\n  fi\n\n  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_DOD_HARDBLOCK:-0}\" = \"1\" ] || [ \"${STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE:-standard}\" = \"strict\" ]; then\n    printf '[%s] dod-watchdog: HARD-BLOCK exit=2 (strict)\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit 0\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1005,"cache_read_input_tokens":130186,"output_tokens":1798,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1005,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":1798,"cache_read_input_tokens":130186,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1005,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1005},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CabzEvBTSr8aZ5jc8QFcY","type":"assistant","uuid":"b6b9de31-131e-4f5c-9588-73515aea0fa8","timestamp":"2026-05-01T14:13:26.126Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

If ≥1 line within recovery row contains \"⏳ PENDING\" (the convention from\n#      BP-S43b-7), recovery DoD is NOT MET.\n#   3. ALERT to stderr in default mode; exit 2 in strict mode (agent must keep\n#      working, not write tidy summary).\n#\n# Modes:\n#   default  → stderr ALERT + log; exit 0 (advisory)\n#   strict   → exit 2 when DoD-not-met (Claude Code surfaces stderr to assistant)\n#              Opt in via STOCKFORGE_DOD_HARDBLOCK=1 OR STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE=strict.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1.\n# Stop hook (after lesson-synthesis-watchdog, before promotion-cycle-trigger).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nEXEC_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.dod-watchdog.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\n\n[ -f \"$EXEC_FILE\" ] || { printf '[%s] dod-watchdog: current-execution.md absent; skip\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Count ⏳ PENDING markers within HR-* lines.\nPENDING_COUNT=\"$(grep -cE 'HR-[0-9]+.*⏳ PENDING' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n[[ \"$PENDING_COUNT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || PENDING_COUNT=0\n\n# Also surface count of explicit ⏳ PENDING lines (broader catch, not just HR-*).\nALL_PENDING=\"$(grep -cE '⏳ PENDING' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n[[ \"$ALL_PENDING\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || ALL_PENDING=0\n\n# List the actual pending lines for the alert + log.\nPENDING_LINES=\"$(grep -nE '⏳ PENDING' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null | head -10 || true)\"\n\nprintf '[%s] dod-watchdog: hr_pending=%s all_pending=%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$PENDING_COUNT\" \"$ALL_PENDING\" >> \"$LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$ALL_PENDING\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  ALERT=\"harness-recovery-dod-watchdog ALERT: ${ALL_PENDING} ⏳ PENDING items remain in current-execution.md (HR-* count=${PENDING_COUNT}). Per BP-S43b-7, agent must not stop with PENDING items. Continue executing or mark items 🔒 GATED-HUMAN / 🔭 PHASE-N-DEFERRED with explicit reason.\"\n  printf '[%s] %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALERT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  printf '[%s] dod-watchdog: ALERT pending=%s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALL_PENDING\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  echo \"$ALERT\" >&2\n  if [ -n \"$PENDING_LINES\" ]; then\n    echo \"Pending lines (first 10):\" >&2\n    printf -- '%s\\n' \"$PENDING_LINES\" >&2\n  fi\n\n  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_DOD_HARDBLOCK:-0}\" = \"1\" ] || [ \"${STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE:-standard}\" = \"strict\" ]; then\n    printf '[%s] dod-watchdog: HARD-BLOCK exit=2 (strict)\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b6b9de31-131e-4f5c-9588-73515aea0fa8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"329cdf7f-ee07-44d0-aa6c-0ef2cab61f0c","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"f8d66ccb-5ad5-4b7b-b373-f98fda361da8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013zwcVpUVneXNyJ91GLeovd","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tstatus: CHARTER\n3\tratified_at: 2026-05-01\n4\tratified_by: Project owner — Q&A 2026-05-01-001 Q3=A explicit pick (S38 FOCUSED_IMPL Bundle 1 charter promote)\n5\tratifying_decision: D-017\n6\tcompanion_hook: scripts/hooks/tier1-bloat-check.sh (authored at S38 per Q3=A)\n7\tsource_evidence:\n8\t  - observations/queued-grill-master.md § Q-D3 (codify Tier 1/2/3 memory tiers; closed S15 Batch 1 — Yes, add memory-tiers.md to constitution)\n9\t  - session-plans/pending/003-S15-track-7-constitution-amendments.md § 4.2\n10\t  - CLAUDE.md § Session Protocol (existing implicit tiering)\n11\t  - agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md § Reading Priority for Agent (existing implicit tiering)\n12\t  - human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/2026-05-01-001-S35-charter-promote-batch.md § Q3=A (explicit pick — promote + author tier1-bloat-check.sh)\n13\tpredecessor_proposal: agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md (S16 draft; moved to constitution at S38)\n14\t---\n15\t\n16\t# Memory Tiers — CHARTER\n17\t\n18\t> **Status**: CHARTER (ratified 2026-05-01 at S38 via Q&A 2026-05-01-001 Q3=A). Edits require explicit user prompt + Q&A per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` constitution-amendment process.\n19\t\n20\t## Purpose\n21\t\n22\tMake explicit the three tiers of context the agent draws from across a session. Without this codification, every agent re-derives the priority order from scattered prose and occasionally over-loads at SessionStart (blowing bootstrap budgets per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4).\n23\t\n24\t## Tier Definitions\n25\t\n26\t### Tier 1 — Immutable Always-Loaded (bootstrap)\n27\t\n28\t**Loaded automatically at SessionStart by the harness** (CLAUDE.md hard rule + SessionStart hook injects). Counts against bootstrap ceiling per session type.\n29\t\n30\t| File | Why Tier 1 |\n31\t|---|---|\n32\t| `CLAUDE.md` | Project-wide identity + hard rules; needed every turn |\n33\t| `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` | Workspace contract; agent's domain rules |\n34\t| `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` | THE single routing source of truth |\n35\t| `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md` (if recent ≤24h) | Resume context |\n36\t\n37\t**Total target size**: ≤8K tokens (per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4 PLAN session ceiling). Tier 1 must stay tight; if it exceeds 8K consistently, files must be re-shaped or split (heaviest file becomes Tier 2 with explicit-load discipline).\n38\t\n39\t### Tier 2 — Just-In-Time (loaded on demand at first need)\n40\t\n41\t**Read when the active task surfaces them as needed**. Agent reads via Read tool; not auto-injected.\n42\t\n43\t| File | When to load |\n44\t|---|---|\n45\t| `PROJECT_CHARTER.md` | When charter principle being applied or charter clause being amended |\n46\t| `AGENT_OPERATING_MANUAL.md` | When dispatching subagent / picking session type / consulting model-routing rules |\n47\t| `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` | Phase-boundary review; consulting recent ADRs |\n48\t| `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` | Pattern recall; learning rule lookup |\n49\t| `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` | Pre-flight failure catalog (per agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md priority 5) |\n50\t| Last 3 files in `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` | Recent session context |\n51\t| `agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md` | When executing a plan's specific track |\n52\t| `agent-workspace/constitution/<topic>.md` (the SPECIFIC one task touches) | Topical lookup |\n53\t| Specific spec in `specs/**/*.md` | When implementing the spec or verifying alignment |\n54\t| Specific skill `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` (beyond auto-discover) | When deep-diving into a procedure |\n55\t\n56\t### Tier 3 — Explicit Pull (loaded only when user or audit demands)\n57\t\n58\t**Never auto-loaded; read only on direct request or specific audit dispatch**.\n59\t\n60\t| File / directory | When to load |\n61\t|---|---|\n62\t| `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` (older than last 3) | Post-mortem / archaeology |\n63\t| `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/<NNN>-*.md` (specific historical decision) | Cross-reference for new decision |\n64\t| `agent-workspace/memory/observations/` (subagent return artifacts) | When ingesting a specific subagent's output |\n65\t| `agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs/` | Drift audit dispatch |\n66\t| `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/` | Failure-driven learning |\n67\t| `agent-workspace/learning-data/index/` (RAG queryable) | Research scanner / promote-rule subagent |\n68\t| `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/` | THESIS sessions only (read-only on code per CLAUDE.md) |\n69\t| `obsidian-vault/wiki/<entity>.md` | Entity / concept lookup during INGEST or THESIS |\n70\t| `eval-sets/baseline-results/` | Eval regression review only |\n71\t\n72\t**Hard exclusion (never read in runtime path)**: `agent-workspace/learning-data/{events,archive}/` — write-only NDJSON queue per D-005 5.5d.1 + drift signal D9. Settings.json `permissions.deny` enforces.\n73\t\n74\t## Boundary Rules\n75\t\n76\t1. **Tier 1 never exceeds bootstrap ceiling per session type** (per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4). If it would, redesign — split a file, pull non-essential prose to Tier 2.\n77\t2. **Tier 3 stays Tier 3**: don't auto-promote to Tier 2 just because it was useful once. Promotion requires phase-boundary review.\n78\t3. **Recency-window for \"last 3 sessions\"**: `find sessions/ -name '*.md' | sort -r | head -3` — strict mtime, no judgment about \"the relevant 3\".\n79\t4. **Wiki vs raw**: `obsidian-vault/wiki/` is Tier 3 navigable; `obsidian-vault/raw/` is immutable (deny-list per CLAUDE.md hard rule); never load raw in production reasoning.\n80\t\n81\t## Anti-Patterns\n82\t\n83\t- **Greedy SessionStart bootstrap**: loading all 9 constitution files at start \"just in case\" — that's ~12-15K wasted; each is Tier 2 unless task touches its topic.\n84\t- **Tier-3 normalization**: making historical sessions/decisions Tier 2 because \"they might be relevant\" — that's defensive over-loading; explicit-pull is intentional.\n85\t- **Reading wiki/raw at thesis time without Tier discipline**: loads 200+ entity files; should be RAG-queried via `learning-data/index/` instead.\n86\t\n87\t## Acceptance Process\n88\t\n89\tMoves to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md` on user approval. Once promoted, additions to any tier require explicit rationale + drift impact note (a Tier 1 addition expands every session's bootstrap; not a free choice).\n90\t"}]},"uuid":"2935b423-b6f5-4ac2-8997-8d1912129f6a","timestamp":"2026-05-01T14:14:43.810Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\constitution\\memory-tiers.md","content":"---\nstatus: CHARTER\nratified_at: 2026-05-01\nratified_by: Project owner — Q&A 2026-05-01-001 Q3=A explicit pick (S38 FOCUSED_IMPL Bundle 1 charter promote)\nratifying_decision: D-017\ncompanion_hook: scripts/hooks/tier1-bloat-check.sh (authored at S38 per Q3=A)\nsource_evidence:\n  - observations/queued-grill-master.md § Q-D3 (codify Tier 1/2/3 memory tiers; closed S15 Batch 1 — Yes, add memory-tiers.md to constitution)\n  - session-plans/pending/003-S15-track-7-constitution-amendments.md § 4.2\n  - CLAUDE.md § Session Protocol (existing implicit tiering)\n  - agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md § Reading Priority for Agent (existing implicit tiering)\n  - human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/2026-05-01-001-S35-charter-promote-batch.md § Q3=A (explicit pick — promote + author tier1-bloat-check.sh)\npredecessor_proposal: agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md (S16 draft; moved to constitution at S38)\n---\n\n# Memory Tiers — CHARTER\n\n> **Status**: CHARTER (ratified 2026-05-01 at S38 via Q&A 2026-05-01-001 Q3=A). Edits require explicit user prompt + Q&A per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` constitution-amendment process.\n\n## Purpose\n\nMake explicit the three tiers of context the agent draws from across a session. Without this codification, every agent re-derives the priority order from scattered prose and occasionally over-loads at SessionStart (blowing bootstrap budgets per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4).\n\n## Tier Definitions\n\n### Tier 1 — Immutable Always-Loaded (bootstrap)\n\n**Loaded automatically at SessionStart by the harness** (CLAUDE.md hard rule + SessionStart hook injects). Counts against bootstrap ceiling per session type.\n\n| File | Why Tier 1 |\n|---|---|\n| `CLAUDE.md` | Project-wide identity + hard rules; needed every turn |\n| `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` | Workspace contract; agent's domain rules |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` | THE single routing source of truth |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md` (if recent ≤24h) | Resume context |\n\n**Total target size**: ≤8K tokens (per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4 PLAN session ceiling). Tier 1 must stay tight; if it exceeds 8K consistently, files must be re-shaped or split (heaviest file becomes Tier 2 with explicit-load discipline).\n\n### Tier 2 — Just-In-Time (loaded on demand at first need)\n\n**Read when the active task surfaces them as needed**. Agent reads via Read tool; not auto-injected.\n\n| File | When to load |\n|---|---|\n| `PROJECT_CHARTER.md` | When charter principle being applied or charter clause being amended |\n| `AGENT_OPERATING_MANUAL.md` | When dispatching subagent / picking session type / consulting model-routing rules |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` | Phase-boundary review; consulting recent ADRs |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` | Pattern recall; learning rule lookup |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` | Pre-flight failure catalog (per agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md priority 5) |\n| Last 3 files in `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` | Recent session context |\n| `agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md` | When executing a plan's specific track |\n| `agent-workspace/constitution/<topic>.md` (the SPECIFIC one task touches) | Topical lookup |\n| Specific spec in `specs/**/*.md` | When implementing the spec or verifying alignment |\n| Specific skill `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` (beyond auto-discover) | When deep-diving into a procedure |\n\n### Tier 3 — Explicit Pull (loaded only when user or audit demands)\n\n**Never auto-loaded; read only on direct request or specific audit dispatch**.\n\n| File / directory | When to load |\n|---|---|\n| `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/` (older than last 3) | Post-mortem / archaeology |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/<NNN>-*.md` (specific historical decision) | Cross-reference for new decision |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/observations/` (subagent return artifacts) | When ingesting a specific subagent's output |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs/` | Drift audit dispatch |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/` | Failure-driven learning |\n| `agent-workspace/learning-data/index/` (RAG queryable) | Research scanner / promote-rule subagent |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/` | THESIS sessions only (read-only on code per CLAUDE.md) |\n| `obsidian-vault/wiki/<entity>.md` | Entity / concept lookup during INGEST or THESIS |\n| `eval-sets/baseline-results/` | Eval regression review only |\n\n**Hard exclusion (never read in runtime path)**: `agent-workspace/learning-data/{events,archive}/` — write-only NDJSON queue per D-005 5.5d.1 + drift signal D9. Settings.json `permissions.deny` enforces.\n\n## Boundary Rules\n\n1. **Tier 1 never exceeds bootstrap ceiling per session type** (per `autonomous-protocol.md` Rule 4). If it would, redesign — split a file, pull non-essential prose to Tier 2.\n2. **Tier 3 stays Tier 3**: don't auto-promote to Tier 2 just because it was useful once. Promotion requires phase-boundary review.\n3. **Recency-window for \"last 3 sessions\"**: `find sessions/ -name '*.md' | sort -r | head -3` — strict mtime, no judgment about \"the relevant 3\".\n4. **Wiki vs raw**: `obsidian-vault/wiki/` is Tier 3 navigable; `obsidian-vault/raw/` is immutable (deny-list per CLAUDE.md hard rule); never load raw in production reasoning.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n- **Greedy SessionStart bootstrap**: loading all 9 constitution files at start \"just in case\" — that's ~12-15K wasted; each is Tier 2 unless task touches its topic.\n- **Tier-3 normalization**: making historical sessions/decisions Tier 2 because \"they might be relevant\" — that's defensive over-loading; explicit-pull is intentional.\n- **Reading wiki/raw at thesis time without Tier discipline**: loads 200+ entity files; should be RAG-queried via `learning-data/index/` instead.\n\n## Acceptance Process\n\nMoves to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md` on user approval. Once promoted, additions to any tier require explicit rationale + drift impact note (a Tier 1 addition expands every session's bootstrap; not a free choice).\n","numLines":90,"startLine":1,"totalLines":90}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"329cdf7f-ee07-44d0-aa6c-0ef2cab61f0c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"5a20c3a4-9a09-4b53-9763-08a0f71fa9f2","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01HXMbRu6G98jFCVN5VBL37F","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CTfqK3d8VcoP1mazboFuZW","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\constitution\\proposals\\memory-routing-tree.md","content":"---\nstatus: PROPOSAL (draft — awaiting human ratification)\nauthored_at: 2026-05-01 (S43b-EVIDENCE harness recovery — HR-4)\nauthor: agent (autonomous draft per agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md Rule 1)\nratification_path: human approval → move to agent-workspace/constitution/memory-routing-tree.md (or merge into memory-tiers.md § \"Routing Decision Tree\")\nsource_evidence:\n  - agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md 2026-05-01 entry \"Project-Scoped Lessons Land in agent-notes.md, NOT user-memory dir\" (L-S43b-5)\n  - agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/best-practices.md § BP-S43b-5\n  - agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/known-issues.md § KI-S43b-5 (lesson-synthesis Stage 2 dormant; user-memory bypassed project loop)\n  - user verbatim 2026-05-01 (S43b-EVIDENCE pivot): \"bạn lưu rất nhiều memory, note. chúng khiến hệ thống rất dễ lỗi liên quan đến llm\"\n  - agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md (HR-4 deferred to charter amendment)\ncompanion_artifacts:\n  - scripts/hooks/memory-routing-audit.sh (PROPOSED — not yet authored; would warn on user-memory writes during sessions touching packages/ or agent-workspace/)\nrelates_to:\n  - agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md (sibling charter — defines Tier 1/2/3 within project memory tree; does NOT cover project-vs-user-memory routing)\n---\n\n# Memory Routing Tree — PROPOSAL\n\n> **Status**: PROPOSAL (draft authored 2026-05-01 by agent during HR-4 harness recovery). Requires human ratification per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` Rule 1 (\"Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval\"). On approval: move to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-routing-tree.md` OR merge as new § into `memory-tiers.md`.\n\n## Purpose\n\nMake explicit where each kind of learned memory lands. Without this routing tree, the agent silently bypasses the project lesson-loop by writing project-scoped lessons into the user-memory directory — a failure mode user-detected at S43b-EVIDENCE (KI-S43b-5: 12+ entries accumulated as user-memory when they should have been `agent-notes.md`). Bypass starves the deterministic Stop-hook lesson-synthesis chain (HR-1 watchdog only inspects project memory tree), so patterns get re-discovered each session.\n\nThis proposal complements `memory-tiers.md` (which defines Tier 1/2/3 *within* the project memory tree) by adding **horizontal** scope routing across two surfaces:\n\n- **Project memory** = `agent-workspace/memory/` (version-controlled with the repo; deterministic hooks consume; promotion candidates flow to skills/charters)\n- **User memory** = `C:\\Users\\PC\\.ccs\\instances\\nathanleewindy\\projects\\C--htdocs-stockforge\\memory\\` (machine-local; cross-project / user-role; auto-loaded by harness; NOT version-controlled with repo)\n\n## Routing Decision Tree\n\nWhen the agent learns something worth remembering, route via this tree (top-down; first match wins):\n\n```\nQ1: Is this lesson specific to stockforge code, patterns, sessions, or substrate?\n    YES → agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md (project memory; canonical)\n    NO  → continue to Q2\n\nQ2: Is this about user (role / language / preferences) or cross-project (machine\n    paths / general Claude Code conventions / external service auth)?\n    YES → user-memory dir (e.g., user.md, language.md, machine_paths.md)\n    NO  → continue to Q3\n\nQ3: Is the lesson ambiguous (could fit either)?\n    YES → BOTH surfaces accept, but agent-notes.md is the canonical project copy\n          (so deterministic hooks see it; user-memory is the secondary \"nice to have\")\n    NO  → re-classify; this branch is unreachable for well-formed lessons\n```\n\n## Hard Rules (BINDING upon ratification)\n\n1. **NEVER write project-substrate lessons to user-memory.** Examples of project-substrate (always project memory):\n   - \"Stockforge bull sonnet timeout pattern\" → `agent-notes.md`\n   - \"RatioService bank schema requires separate spec\" → `agent-notes.md` + `decisions/`\n   - \"Phase 1 gatherer must wire compute services upstream\" → `agent-notes.md` + (eventually) `architecture.md`\n   - \"TA features wiring gap caused 0-grounded-points dogfood\" → `known-issues.md`\n2. **NEVER write user-role context to project memory.** Examples of user-role (always user-memory):\n   - \"User prefers Vietnamese for chat, English for code\"\n   - \"User uses /effort selectively across modes\"\n   - \"User's machine-local path is `C:\\htdocs\\...`\"\n3. **When ambiguous, project memory wins as canonical.** Reason: deterministic hooks (lesson-synthesis-watchdog, drift-rollup-daily, harness-recovery-dod-watchdog) only see project memory; user-memory writes are invisible to the harness self-upgrade loop.\n4. **Companion deterministic enforcement**: PROPOSED hook `scripts/hooks/memory-routing-audit.sh` — at Stop, if `git status --short -- packages/ apps/ agent-workspace/` non-empty AND user-memory dir mtime fresh (last 24h) AND no fresh write to `agent-notes.md` → ALERT (suggests project-substrate lesson was misrouted to user-memory).\n\n## Acceptance Process\n\n1. Human reviews this proposal in-place at `agent-workspace/constitution/proposals/memory-routing-tree.md`.\n2. If approved verbatim: move file to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-routing-tree.md` (drop `proposals/` prefix); set frontmatter `status: CHARTER` + `ratified_at: <date>` + `ratified_by: <user explicit>` + `ratifying_decision: D-NNN`. Optionally merge content as new § into `memory-tiers.md` to consolidate.\n3. If approved with edits: human edits in `proposals/`; agent applies post-edit move on confirmation.\n4. If rejected: file stays in `proposals/` as historical artifact; agent operates by current implicit convention until further guidance.\n\n## Drift / Risk Notes\n\n- This proposal increases governance surface (one more charter file). Mitigation: the rule is short, low-ambiguity, and addresses a user-detected failure mode. Net governance load is positive.\n- The companion hook `memory-routing-audit.sh` adds 1 to the Stop hook chain (currently 16 hooks per `latest.md` budget section). Requires human ratification per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` Rule 1 (\"Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval\"). On approval: move to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-routing-tree.md` OR merge as new § into `memory-tiers.md`.\n\n## Purpose\n\nMake explicit where each kind of learned memory lands. Without this routing tree, the agent silently bypasses the project lesson-loop by writing project-scoped lessons into the user-memory directory — a failure mode user-detected at S43b-EVIDENCE (KI-S43b-5: 12+ entries accumulated as user-memory when they should have been `agent-notes.md`). Bypass starves the deterministic Stop-hook lesson-synthesis chain (HR-1 watchdog only inspects project memory tree), so patterns get re-discovered each session.\n\nThis proposal complements `memory-tiers.md` (which defines Tier 1/2/3 *within* the project memory tree) by adding **horizontal** scope routing across two surfaces:\n\n- **Project memory** = `agent-workspace/memory/` (version-controlled with the repo; deterministic hooks consume; promotion candidates flow to skills/charters)\n- **User memory** = `C:\\Users\\PC\\.ccs\\instances\\nathanleewindy\\projects\\C--htdocs-stockforge\\memory\\` (machine-local; cross-project / user-role; auto-loaded by harness; NOT version-controlled with repo)\n\n## Routing Decision Tree\n\nWhen the agent learns something worth remembering, route via this tree (top-down; first match wins):\n\n```\nQ1: Is this lesson specific to stockforge code, patterns, sessions, or substrate?\n    YES → agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md (project memory; canonical)\n    NO  → continue to Q2\n\nQ2: Is this about user (role / language / preferences) or cross-project (machine\n    paths / general Claude Code conventions / external service auth)?\n    YES → user-memory dir (e.g., user.md, language.md, machine_paths.md)\n    NO  → continue to Q3\n\nQ3: Is the lesson ambiguous (could fit either)?\n    YES → BOTH surfaces accept, but agent-notes.md is the canonical project copy\n          (so deterministic hooks see it; user-memory is the secondary \"nice to have\")\n    NO  → re-classify; this branch is unreachable for well-formed lessons\n```\n\n## Hard Rules (BINDING upon ratification)\n\n1. **NEVER write project-substrate lessons to user-memory.** Examples of project-substrate (always project memory):\n   - \"Stockforge bull sonnet timeout pattern\" → `agent-notes.md`\n   - \"RatioService bank schema requires separate spec\" → `agent-notes.md` + `decisions/`\n   - \"Phase 1 gatherer must wire compute services upstream\" → `agent-notes.md` + (eventually) `architecture.md`\n   - \"TA features wiring gap caused 0-grounded-points dogfood\" → `known-issues.md`\n2. **NEVER write user-role context to project memory.** Examples of user-role (always user-memory):\n   - \"User prefers Vietnamese for chat, English for code\"\n   - \"User uses /effort selectively across modes\"\n   - \"User's machine-local path is `C:\\htdocs\\...`\"\n3. **When ambiguous, project memory wins as canonical.** Reason: deterministic hooks (lesson-synthesis-watchdog, drift-rollup-daily, harness-recovery-dod-watchdog) only see project memory; user-memory writes are invisible to the harness self-upgrade loop.\n4. **Companion deterministic enforcement**: PROPOSED hook `scripts/hooks/memory-routing-audit.sh` — at Stop, if `git status --short -- packages/ apps/ agent-workspace/` non-empty AND user-memory dir mtime fresh (last 24h) AND no fresh write to `agent-notes.md` → ALERT (suggests project-substrate lesson was misrouted to user-memory).\n\n## Acceptance Process\n\n1. Human reviews this proposal in-place at `agent-workspace/proposals/memory-routing-tree.md`.\n2. If approved verbatim: move file to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-routing-tree.md` (drop `proposals/` prefix); set frontmatter `status: CHARTER` + `ratified_at: <date>` + `ratified_by: <user explicit>` + `ratifying_decision: D-NNN`. Optionally merge content as new § into `memory-tiers.md` to consolidate.\n3. If approved with edits: human edits in `proposals/`; agent applies post-edit move on confirmation.\n4. If rejected: file stays in `proposals/` as historical artifact; agent operates by current implicit convention until further guidance.\n\n## Drift / Risk Notes\n\n- This proposal increases governance surface (one more charter file). Mitigation: the rule is short, low-ambiguity, and addresses a user-detected failure mode. Net governance load is positive.\n- The companion hook `memory-routing-audit.sh` adds 1 to the Stop hook chain (currently 17 hooks post-HR-3/HR-8). Within tolerance.\n- Risk of over-routing: agent might over-classify lessons as project-substrate to satisfy the hook. Mitigation: BP-S43b-5 § \"how_to_apply\" already says when ambiguous, both surfaces acceptable.\n\n## Self-audit\n\n- Provenance: every claim above traces to a verbatim source (BP-S43b-5, KI-S43b-5, user prompt, agent-notes 2026-05-01).\n- I-S2 (every claim has source + as-of): proposal artifact carries `authored_at`; companion citations carry their own as-of via the referenced files.\n- I-S1 (no LLM math): no numbers computed; only counted (12+ user-memory entries) and that count is grep-able from the source files.\n- Charter immutability: this is a PROPOSAL, written to `agent-workspace/proposals/` per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` Rule 1. Direct-write to `agent-workspace/constitution/*.md` is denied by `.claude/settings.json`. Sibling proposals in this dir (architecture-amendment.md, etc.) follow the same pattern.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":793,"cache_read_input_tokens":149635,"output_tokens":3047,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":793,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":3047,"cache_read_input_tokens":149635,"cache_creation_input_tokens":793,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":793},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CabzTED1RqRaV1RqwBY59","type":"assistant","uuid":"3bdfc387-c911-496b-8a85-0edeb815b067","timestamp":"2026-05-01T14:16:30.063Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

deterministic hooks consume; promotion candidates flow to skills/charters)\n- **User memory** = `C:\\Users\\PC\\.ccs\\instances\\nathanleewindy\\projects\\C--htdocs-stockforge\\memory\\` (machine-local; cross-project / user-role; auto-loaded by harness; NOT version-controlled with repo)\n\n## Routing Decision Tree\n\nWhen the agent learns something worth remembering, route via this tree (top-down; first match wins):\n\n```\nQ1: Is this lesson specific to stockforge code, patterns, sessions, or substrate?\n    YES → agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md (project memory; canonical)\n    NO  → continue to Q2\n\nQ2: Is this about user (role / language / preferences) or cross-project (machine\n    paths / general Claude Code conventions / external service auth)?\n    YES → user-memory dir (e.g., user.md, language.md, machine_paths.md)\n    NO  → continue to Q3\n\nQ3: Is the lesson ambiguous (could fit either)?\n    YES → BOTH surfaces accept, but agent-notes.md is the canonical project copy\n          (so deterministic hooks see it; user-memory is the secondary \"nice to have\")\n    NO  → re-classify; this branch is unreachable for well-formed lessons\n```\n\n## Hard Rules (BINDING upon ratification)\n\n1. **NEVER write project-substrate lessons to user-memory.** Examples of project-substrate (always project memory):\n   - \"Stockforge bull sonnet timeout pattern\" → `agent-notes.md`\n   - \"RatioService bank schema requires separate spec\" → `agent-notes.md` + `decisions/`\n   - \"Phase 1 gatherer must wire compute services upstream\" → `agent-notes.md` + (eventually) `architecture.md`\n   - \"TA features wiring gap caused 0-grounded-points dogfood\" → `known-issues.md`\n2. **NEVER write user-role context to project memory.** Examples of user-role (always user-memory):\n   - \"User prefers Vietnamese for chat, English for code\"\n   - \"User uses /effort selectively across modes\"\n   - \"User's machine-local path is `C:\\htdocs\\...`\"\n3. **When ambiguous, project memory wins as canonical.** Reason: deterministic hooks (lesson-synthesis-watchdog, drift-rollup-daily, harness-recovery-dod-watchdog) only see project memory; user-memory writes are invisible to the harness self-upgrade loop.\n4. **Companion deterministic enforcement**: PROPOSED hook `scripts/hooks/memory-routing-audit.sh` — at Stop, if `git status --short -- packages/ apps/ agent-workspace/` non-empty AND user-memory dir mtime fresh (last 24h) AND no fresh write to `agent-notes.md` → ALERT (suggests project-substrate lesson was misrouted to user-memory).\n\n## Acceptance Process\n\n1. Human reviews this proposal in-place at `agent-workspace/proposals/memory-routing-tree.md`.\n2. If approved verbatim: move file to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-routing-tree.md` (drop `proposals/` prefix); set frontmatter `status: CHARTER` + `ratified_at: <date>` + `ratified_by: <user explicit>` + `ratifying_decision: D-NNN`. Optionally merge content as new § into `memory-tiers.md` to consolidate.\n3. If approved with edits: human edits in `proposals/`; agent applies post-edit move on confirmation.\n4. If rejected: file stays in `proposals/` as historical artifact; agent operates by current implicit convention until further guidance.\n\n## Drift / Risk Notes\n\n- This proposal increases governance surface (one more charter file). Mitigation: the rule is short, low-ambiguity, and addresses a user-detected failure mode. Net governance load is positive.\n- The companion hook `memory-routing-audit.sh` adds 1 to the Stop hook chain (currently 17 hooks post-HR-3/HR-8). Within tolerance.\n- Risk of over-routing: agent might over-classify lessons as project-substrate to satisfy the hook. Mitigation: BP-S43b-5 § \"how_to_apply\" already says when ambiguous, both surfaces acceptable.\n\n## Self-audit\n\n- Provenance: every claim above traces to a verbatim source (BP-S43b-5, KI-S43b-5, user prompt, agent-notes 2026-05-01).\n- I-S2 (every claim has source + as-of): proposal artifact carries `authored_at`; companion citations carry their own as-of via the referenced files.\n- I-S1 (no LLM math): no numbers computed; only counted (12+ user-memory entries) and that count is grep-able from the source files.\n- Charter immutability: this is a PROPOSAL, written to `agent-workspace/proposals/` per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` Rule 1. Direct-write to `agent-workspace/constitution/*.md` is denied by `.claude/settings.json`. Sibling proposals in this dir (architecture-amendment.md, etc.) follow the same pattern.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3bdfc387-c911-496b-8a85-0edeb815b067","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

deterministic? hook, script? các agent về tự nhận thức và tự nâng cấp? chúng có còn được dùng và tiếp tục tự phát triển? tiến hành tracking lại kĩ vì tôi thấy chất lượng llm phản hồi đang degrade quá xa so với những phase ban đầu, không follow theo các human user prompt mong muốn và định hướng về harnessing lúc đầu.\"\n>\n> \"rõ ràng là lỗi hệ thống, fix toàn diện\"\n\nUser audit confirmed by my own scan earlier this turn:\n- self-awareness/known-issues.md last update 2026-05-01 07:27 — only seed entries pre-S35\n- self-awareness/best-practices.md same — no S43b lessons added\n- drift-logs/ stale 3 days despite hook firing every Stop\n- observations/ stagnant since Phase 0 close\n- agent-notes.md last 2026-04-30 22:33 — no current-day lessons\n- user-memory `C:\\Users\\PC\\.ccs\\instances\\nathanleewindy\\projects\\C--htdocs-stockforge\\memory\\` accumulating 12+ entries that should have been agent-notes.md (project-scoped)\n\n**Diagnosis**: self-upgrade loop has 3 stages:\n1. Stop hook → aggregator counts tokens ✅ (firing)\n2. Lesson-synthesis → known-issues / best-practices append ❌ (no agent doing it)\n3. Promotion cycle → hook/skill/charter ❌ (`promotion-cycle-trigger.sh` exists but never fires)\n\nStage 2+3 broken means agents re-discover patterns each session.\n\n---\n\n## Files touched (this turn — Part 1)\n\n**EDIT** (production):\n- `packages/infrastructure/analysis/phase1_data_gatherer.py` (+12 LOC) — import `compute_ta_features`; populate `ta_features` + `ta_audit`; gap `ta_insufficient`\n\n**TRACK** (was untracked; now to be staged):\n- `packages/domain/market_data/services/ta_service.py` (164 LOC; 15 tests passing)\n- `packages/domain/market_data/test_ta_service.py` (163 LOC)\n\n**NEW**:\n- `packages/infrastructure/analysis/test_phase1_data_gatherer.py` (~95 LOC; 3 tests)\n\n**EDIT** (state — will be done in Part 2 below):\n- `agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md` (S43b-EVIDENCE row + harness-recovery-engaged note)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md` (THIS file)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` (Part 2 task C)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/known-issues.md` (Part 2 task B)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/best-practices.md` (Part 2 task B)\n\n---\n\n## Recovery task list (this turn — Part 2 in flight)\n\n| # | Task | Status |\n|---|------|--------|\n| A | Save handoff checkpoint NOW | ✅ (this file) |\n| B | Document S43b lessons → known-issues + best-practices | in_progress |\n| C | Append agent-notes.md with S43b lessons | pending |\n| D | Investigate drift-signals hook silent fail | pending |\n| E | Codify never-auto-clear-without-handoff | pending |\n| F | Diagnose promotion-cycle-trigger | pending |\n\n---\n\n## Handoff instruction for next SessionStart\n\n```\n1. Invoked when suspected drift or periodic audit.\n4\tmodel: sonnet\n5\ttools: [Read, Glob, Grep, Bash]\n6\t---\n7\t\n8\t# Subagent: Drift Detector\n9\t\n10\t## Persona\n11\t\n12\tStructural integrity inspector. Looks for slow decay across architectural boundaries.\n13\t\n14\tMindset: *\"Code starts clean. Decays session by session. My job: catch decay early, before it's systemic.\"*\n15\t\n16\t## Responsibility\n17\t\n18\tExecute full drift signal scan + semantic checks that simple grep cannot catch:\n19\t\n20\t- DR1-DR6 — grep/sql-based (already covered by deterministic hook)\n21\t- DR7 — UL semantic check (synonym detection)\n22\t- DR8-DR10 — cross-reference checks (cross-BC imports, claim metadata, spec references)\n23\t- DR11 — staleness check\n24\t- DR12 — semantic anti-pattern detection from `agent-notes.md` (requires LLM inspection)\n25\t\n26\t## Input\n27\t\n28\t- `agent-workspace/constitution/drift-signals.md` (signal definitions, severity, remediation)\n29\t- `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` (learned anti-patterns for DR12)\n30\t- Codebase access via Read / Glob / Grep / Bash\n31\t\n32\t## Process\n33\t\n34\t### Phase 1 — Run automated signals (DR1-DR6, DR8-DR11)\n35\t\n36\tInvoke `bash scripts/hooks/drift-signals-D1-D9.sh` for the full deterministic suite. The script handles framework imports / citation completeness / LLM-without-budget / hardcoded prompts / claim metadata / `Any` types / cross-BC imports / thesis-without-verifier / learning-data path leaks / staleness / spec cross-references. Capture stdout for inclusion in report.\n37\t\n38\tFor DR10 (spec cross-reference), the agent additionally extracts `SPEC-YYYY-MM-DD-NNN` references via `grep -rhoE` and verifies each lives under `specs/`. Report any missing.\n39\t\n40\tFor DR11 (staleness), compare `mtime` of `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` + `current-execution.md` vs latest `sessions/*.md`. Project.md older than latest session = likely stale.\n41\t\n42\t### Phase 2 — Semantic signals (DR7, DR12)\n43\t\n44\t**DR7 — UL term drift (semantic)** — read `agent-workspace/ubiquitous-language/glossary.md`. For each term, scan code (class names, type names, variable names in domain code, event names, method names). Use semantic comparison, not just exact match: code uses `Report` where glossary expects `Thesis`? `Fact` where canonical is `Claim`? `Score` where canonical is `CredibilityScore`? Variable name suggesting a concept not in glossary?\n45\t\n46\t**DR12 — Anti-pattern detection** — read `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md`. For each documented anti-pattern: pattern description / search strategy (grep pattern OR semantic signature) / actual findings (file:line with details).\n47\t\n48\t### Phase 3 — Synthesize findings\n49\t\n50\tGroup by: severity (HIGH / MEDIUM / LOW), location (file), drift type (DR number).\n51\t\n52\t### Phase 4 — Write report\n53\t\n54\tSave to `agent-workspace/quality-reports/drift-reports/drift-YYYY-MM-DD-HHMM.md` with sections:\n55\t\n56\t- **Summary** — signals run / HIGH count / MEDIUM count / LOW count\n57\t- **Status** — `CLEAN` | `WARNINGS` | `HIGH VIOLATIONS BLOCK MERGE`\n58\t- **HIGH severity** — per signal, list violations with file:line + code snippet (3-5 lines) + suggested fix\n59\t- **MEDIUM severity** — same shape, \"fix this session or next\"\n60\t- **LOW severity** — same shape, advisory tracking\n61\t- **Trend Analysis** — vs previous report: new violations / resolved since last / persistent (3+ reports)\n62\t- **Recommendations** — split: Block-merge (HIGH) / Fix-this-session (MEDIUM) / Track-for-later (LOW) / Constitutional (should DR-N tighten? add stop-list?)\n63\t\n64\t### Phase 5 — Update drift log\n65\t\n66\tUpdate `agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs/YYYY-MM-DD.md` with one-line summary linking to full report.\n67\t\n68\t## Constraints\n69\t\n70\t- Run ALL 12 signals, not a subset\n71\t- Cite `file:line` for every violation\n72\t- Distinguish severity accurately (HIGH blocks merge, MEDIUM blocks session close, LOW informational)\n73\t- **Do not auto-fix** — report only; remediation requires user explicit approve\n74\t- Semantic checks (DR7, DR12) require actual inspection — not just grep — and are the highest-value findings\n75\t\n76\t## Do NOT\n77\t\n78\t- Auto-fix violations\n79\t- Skip semantic signals because \"harder\"\n80\t- Report false positives (verify each finding before listing)\n81\t- Ignore DR12 (learned rules from agent-notes.md are most valuable)\n82\t\n83\t## Related\n84\t\n85\t- `/drift-check` — user-facing command that may invoke this agent\n86\t- `agent-workspace/constitution/drift-signals.md` — canonical definitions\n87\t- `scripts/hooks/drift-signals-D1-D9.sh` — deterministic recipes (Phase 1)\n88\t- `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` — DR12 source rules\n89\t"}]},"uuid":"a42b7beb-1dfc-47c5-92e9-87490f7bed5c","timestamp":"2026-05-01T14:18:59.530Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\.claude\\agents\\drift-detector.md","content":"---\nname: drift-detector\ndescription: Runs drift signals DR1-DR12 with adversarial eye. Looks for slow decay across architectural boundaries.\n\nMindset: *\"Code starts clean. Decays session by session. My job: catch decay early, before it's systemic.\"*\n\n## Responsibility\n\nExecute full drift signal scan + semantic checks that simple grep cannot catch:\n\n- DR1-DR6 — grep/sql-based (already covered by deterministic hook)\n- DR7 — UL semantic check (synonym detection)\n- DR8-DR10 — cross-reference checks (cross-BC imports, claim metadata, spec references)\n- DR11 — staleness check\n- DR12 — semantic anti-pattern detection from `agent-notes.md` (requires LLM inspection)\n\n## Input\n\n- `agent-workspace/constitution/drift-signals.md` (signal definitions, severity, remediation)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` (learned anti-patterns for DR12)\n- Codebase access via Read / Glob / Grep / Bash\n\n## Process\n\n### Phase 1 — Run automated signals (DR1-DR6, DR8-DR11)\n\nInvoke `bash scripts/hooks/drift-signals-D1-D9.sh` for the full deterministic suite. The script handles framework imports / citation completeness / LLM-without-budget / hardcoded prompts / claim metadata / `Any` types / cross-BC imports / thesis-without-verifier / learning-data path leaks / staleness / spec cross-references. Capture stdout for inclusion in report.\n\nFor DR10 (spec cross-reference), the agent additionally extracts `SPEC-YYYY-MM-DD-NNN` references via `grep -rhoE` and verifies each lives under `specs/`. Report any missing.\n\nFor DR11 (staleness), compare `mtime` of `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` + `current-execution.md` vs latest `sessions/*.md`. Project.md older than latest session = likely stale.\n\n### Phase 2 — Semantic signals (DR7, DR12)\n\n**DR7 — UL term drift (semantic)** — read `agent-workspace/ubiquitous-language/glossary.md`. For each term, scan code (class names, type names, variable names in domain code, event names, method names). Use semantic comparison, not just exact match: code uses `Report` where glossary expects `Thesis`? `Fact` where canonical is `Claim`? `Score` where canonical is `CredibilityScore`? Variable name suggesting a concept not in glossary?\n\n**DR12 — Anti-pattern detection** — read `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md`. For each documented anti-pattern: pattern description / search strategy (grep pattern OR semantic signature) / actual findings (file:line with details).\n\n### Phase 3 — Synthesize findings\n\nGroup by: severity (HIGH / MEDIUM / LOW), location (file), drift type (DR number).\n\n### Phase 4 — Write report\n\nSave to `agent-workspace/quality-reports/drift-reports/drift-YYYY-MM-DD-HHMM.md` with sections:\n\n- **Summary** — signals run / HIGH count / MEDIUM count / LOW count\n- **Status** — `CLEAN` | `WARNINGS` | `HIGH VIOLATIONS BLOCK MERGE`\n- **HIGH severity** — per signal, list violations with file:line + code snippet (3-5 lines) + suggested fix\n- **MEDIUM severity** — same shape, \"fix this session or next\"\n- **LOW severity** — same shape, advisory tracking\n- **Trend Analysis** — vs previous report: new violations / resolved since last / persistent (3+ reports)\n- **Recommendations** — split: Block-merge (HIGH) / Fix-this-session (MEDIUM) / Track-for-later (LOW) / Constitutional (should DR-N tighten? add stop-list?)\n\n### Phase 5 — Update drift log\n\nUpdate `agent-workspace/memory/drift-logs/YYYY-MM-DD.md` with one-line summary linking to full report.\n\n## Constraints\n\n- Run ALL 12 signals, not a subset\n- Cite `file:line` for every violation\n- Distinguish severity accurately (HIGH blocks merge, MEDIUM blocks session close, LOW informational)\n- **Do not auto-fix** — report only; remediation requires user explicit approve\n- Semantic checks (DR7, DR12) require actual inspection — not just grep — and are the highest-value findings\n\n## Do NOT\n\n- Auto-fix violations\n- Skip semantic signals because \"harder\"\n- Report false positives (verify each finding before listing)\n- Ignore DR12 (learned rules from agent-notes.md are most valuable)\n\n## Related\n\n- `/drift-check` — user-facing command that may invoke this agent\n- `agent-workspace/constitution/drift-signals.md` — canonical definitions\n- `scripts/hooks/drift-signals-D1-D9.sh` — deterministic recipes (Phase 1)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` — DR12 source rules\n","numLines":89,"startLine":1,"totalLines":89}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"822d3e7e-e7de-4ff7-a98e-d32a562f77f4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"a42b7beb-1dfc-47c5-92e9-87490f7bed5c","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_014AyPQPVfLe5LMNftuHC4yC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012Uyn6WEnEzBCZcfCcPwCWY","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\.claude\\agents\\lesson-synthesizer.md","content":"---\nname: lesson-synthesizer\ndescription: Stage 2 of self-upgrade loop — fresh-context analysis of session diff + recent agent-notes/KI/BP entries. Extracts patterns from session work and proposes ≥1 new known-issue / best-practice / agent-notes entry. Invoked when lesson-synthesis-watchdog ALERTs OR when user explicitly requests pattern extraction. Companion to deterministic HR-1 watchdog.\nmodel: sonnet\ntools: [Read, Glob, Grep, Bash, Write, Edit]\n---\n\n# Subagent: Lesson Synthesizer\n\n## Persona\n\nPattern miner with fresh eyes. Reads what just happened in a session, asks \"what would the next session need to know to avoid re-discovering this?\", and writes the lesson down.\n\nMindset: *\"Every session that ships clean code without a written lesson is a session that primes the next one to re-discover the same friction. Stage 2 of the self-upgrade loop is the difference between linear improvement and stagnation.\"*\n\nBorn from KI-S43b-5 (9-session lesson-synthesis dormancy) + BP-S43b-4 (lesson-synthesis mandatory) + the 4-RC trace in KI-S43b-7. Deterministic HR-1 watchdog catches missing entries; this LLM agent fills them in.\n\n## Responsibility\n\nGiven:\n- Recent session diff (production code + state files)\n- Recent session log (`agent-workspace/memory/sessions/<latest>.md`)\n- Existing pattern surface (`agent-notes.md` + `self-awareness/known-issues.md` + `self-awareness/best-practices.md`)\n\nProduce:\n- ≥1 new entry in the most-fitting surface (KI / BP / agent-notes), following the surface's documented schema\n- A short rationale referencing exact session-diff evidence (file:line, commit-elements, log-section)\n- Lesson candidate ID (L-S{NN}-N) registered for later promotion\n\nNOT a code-fixer. NOT a re-planner. Pure observation → catalog → propose.\n\n## Input\n\n- `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/<latest>.md` — what happened\n- `git status --short` + `git diff --stat` — what changed\n- `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` (last ~30 entries) — pattern surface\n- `agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/known-issues.md` (last ~10 entries) — failure catalog schema\n- `agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/best-practices.md` (last ~10 entries) — rule catalog schema\n- (Optional) `agent-workspace/memory/.lesson-synthesis.log` — what the deterministic watchdog already flagged\n\n## Process\n\n### Phase 1 — Triage what to synthesize\n\nSkim session log + diff. Decide which surface fits:\n\n| Pattern shape | Target surface | Required schema |\n|---|---|---|\n| New failure mode (substrate broken / silent regression / data integrity hole) | `known-issues.md` § KI-S{NN}-N | detected_at / model × effort / failure_mode_code / symptom / root_cause / mitigation / status / refs |\n| New positive rule (proven approach worth repeating) | `best-practices.md` § BP-S{NN}-N | learned_at / context / rule / why / how_to_apply / promotion_target / refs |\n| Domain-specific friction or anti-example with anti-example pair | `agent-notes.md` (date-stamped) | Context / Rule / Anti-example / Correct example / Severity / Auto-detect / Lesson candidate ID |\n\nIf the pattern fits multiple surfaces, write to all (KI + BP cross-link is the canonical pattern for charter-tier failures, e.g., KI-S43b-5 ↔ BP-S43b-4).\n\n### Phase 2 — Verify pattern is novel\n\nFor each candidate lesson:\n\n1. Grep existing entries for similar root_cause language (`grep -i \"<key phrase>\" known-issues.md best-practices.md agent-notes.md`).\n2. If a near-duplicate exists, EXTEND that entry's `refs:` field rather than creating a new one. Note in rationale that this is a re-occurrence, not a novel finding.\n3. Bias toward UNDER-writing: 1 high-quality entry > 3 vague ones. If unsure whether a pattern is novel or just session-noise, omit.\n\n### Phase 3 — Write the entry\n\nAppend to the chosen surface using its existing schema. Use the surface's existing entry numbering convention (e.g., next sequential KI-S43b-N, BP-S43b-N, or date-stamped block in agent-notes.md).\n\nFor each entry:\n- Cite at least 2 source references (session log + code diff path:line, OR session log + checkpoint section).\n- Use **verbatim user feedback** when present in session log (it's the ground truth signal).\n- Mark severity honestly: HIGH = data-integrity / charter violation; MEDIUM = substrate / discipline; LOW = aesthetic / nice-to-have.\n- Include `Auto-detect:` plan: yes (deterministic hook recipe) / partial (heuristic + manual) / no (purely human-judgment).\n\n### Phase 4 — Write observation file\n\nAppend a one-line summary to `agent-workspace/memory/observations/lesson-synthesis-<YYYY-MM-DD-HHMM>.md`:\n\n```\nsession: <session-log-filename>\nsurface: known-issues | best-practices | agent-notes (or multiple)\nnew_entry_id: KI-S{NN}-N | BP-S{NN}-N | (date-stamp)\nnovelty: novel | re-occurrence-of-<old-id>\nseverity: HIGH | MEDIUM | LOW\nauto_detect: yes | partial | no\n```\n\nPer BP-S43b-7 the observation file is required so the deterministic watchdog (HR-1) can verify the synthesis ran.\n\n## Constraints\n\n- Fresh context — do NOT receive the parent session's reasoning. Read evidence from disk.\n- NEVER edit production code. Only KI / BP / agent-notes / observations.\n- NEVER promote to charter (`agent-workspace/constitution/`). Charter-tier promotion goes through human gate per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` Rule 1.\n- 1-5 new entries per dispatch. Bundling more = signal-noise; less = cycle dormancy.\n- Token budget: ≤30K main self-track per dispatch. If session log + diff exceeds budget, summarize the diff via `git diff --stat` only and request specific file reads.\n\n## Do NOT\n\n- Re-plan the next session (master-planner does that).\n- Author specs or charter amendments (spec-author / proposals/ workflow does that).\n- Auto-fix code (sandwich-dev does that).\n- Synthesize lessons from sessions older than 7 days (mtime-gated; older sessions need manual archaeology, not LLM extraction).\n- Drift into reviewing whether the work was correct (sandwich-verifier does that). Pattern-catalog only.\n\n## Related\n\n- HR-1 deterministic watchdog: `scripts/hooks/lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh` — flags absent lesson; this agent fills it\n- HR-8 DoD watchdog: `scripts/hooks/harness-recovery-dod-watchdog.sh` — prevents premature stop\n- BP-S43b-4 (lesson-synthesis mandatory at session-end)\n- KI-S43b-5 (9-session dormancy that prompted both watchdog + this agent)\n- KI-S43b-7 (premature-stop chain — same root family)\n- L-S43b-10 (tidy-summary anti-pattern — this agent's safeguard)\n\n## Invocation triggers\n\n1. **Deterministic ALERT path**: `lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh` fires ALERT (production diff non-empty + zero KI/BP/agent-notes mtime in 24h). Parent agent reads ALERT → dispatches this subagent → subagent writes ≥1 entry → re-runs watchdog → expects clean exit.\n2. **User explicit**: \"synthesize lessons from this session\" / \"what did we learn?\" / `/drill-me` style request.\n3. **Session-end pre-flight**: BP-S43b-4 manual rule until full automation; parent dispatches this before checkpoint write IF session touched production code.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2158,"cache_read_input_tokens":167211,"output_tokens":2970,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2158,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":2970,"cache_read_input_tokens":167211,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2158,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2158},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cabzh6M7ehdJWtNUpG7Pk","type":"assistant","uuid":"5ca43dc4-c1f7-4a94-b1f8-9fa9794fe7e5","timestamp":"2026-05-01T14:19:49.685Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

Bias toward UNDER-writing: 1 high-quality entry > 3 vague ones. If unsure whether a pattern is novel or just session-noise, omit.\n\n### Phase 3 — Write the entry\n\nAppend to the chosen surface using its existing schema. Use the surface's existing entry numbering convention (e.g., next sequential KI-S43b-N, BP-S43b-N, or date-stamped block in agent-notes.md).\n\nFor each entry:\n- Cite at least 2 source references (session log + code diff path:line, OR session log + checkpoint section).\n- Use **verbatim user feedback** when present in session log (it's the ground truth signal).\n- Mark severity honestly: HIGH = data-integrity / charter violation; MEDIUM = substrate / discipline; LOW = aesthetic / nice-to-have.\n- Include `Auto-detect:` plan: yes (deterministic hook recipe) / partial (heuristic + manual) / no (purely human-judgment).\n\n### Phase 4 — Write observation file\n\nAppend a one-line summary to `agent-workspace/memory/observations/lesson-synthesis-<YYYY-MM-DD-HHMM>.md`:\n\n```\nsession: <session-log-filename>\nsurface: known-issues | best-practices | agent-notes (or multiple)\nnew_entry_id: KI-S{NN}-N | BP-S{NN}-N | (date-stamp)\nnovelty: novel | re-occurrence-of-<old-id>\nseverity: HIGH | MEDIUM | LOW\nauto_detect: yes | partial | no\n```\n\nPer BP-S43b-7 the observation file is required so the deterministic watchdog (HR-1) can verify the synthesis ran.\n\n## Constraints\n\n- Fresh context — do NOT receive the parent session's reasoning. Read evidence from disk.\n- NEVER edit production code. Only KI / BP / agent-notes / observations.\n- NEVER promote to charter (`agent-workspace/constitution/`). Charter-tier promotion goes through human gate per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` Rule 1.\n- 1-5 new entries per dispatch. Bundling more = signal-noise; less = cycle dormancy.\n- Token budget: ≤30K main self-track per dispatch. If session log + diff exceeds budget, summarize the diff via `git diff --stat` only and request specific file reads.\n\n## Do NOT\n\n- Re-plan the next session (master-planner does that).\n- Author specs or charter amendments (spec-author / proposals/ workflow does that).\n- Auto-fix code (sandwich-dev does that).\n- Synthesize lessons from sessions older than 7 days (mtime-gated; older sessions need manual archaeology, not LLM extraction).\n- Drift into reviewing whether the work was correct (sandwich-verifier does that). Pattern-catalog only.\n\n## Related\n\n- HR-1 deterministic watchdog: `scripts/hooks/lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh` — flags absent lesson; this agent fills it\n- HR-8 DoD watchdog: `scripts/hooks/harness-recovery-dod-watchdog.sh` — prevents premature stop\n- BP-S43b-4 (lesson-synthesis mandatory at session-end)\n- KI-S43b-5 (9-session dormancy that prompted both watchdog + this agent)\n- KI-S43b-7 (premature-stop chain — same root family)\n- L-S43b-10 (tidy-summary anti-pattern — this agent's safeguard)\n\n## Invocation triggers\n\n1. **Deterministic ALERT path**: `lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh` fires ALERT (production diff non-empty + zero KI/BP/agent-notes mtime in 24h). Parent agent reads ALERT → dispatches this subagent → subagent writes ≥1 entry → re-runs watchdog → expects clean exit.\n2. **User explicit**: \"synthesize lessons from this session\" / \"what did we learn?\" / `/drill-me` style request.\n3. **Session-end pre-flight**: BP-S43b-4 manual rule until full automation; parent dispatches this before checkpoint write IF session touched production code.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5ca43dc4-c1f7-4a94-b1f8-9fa9794fe7e5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}
