AUTOMATED PATH: dispatch \\`lesson-synthesizer\\` subagent (.claude/agents/lesson-synthesizer.md) to extract patterns from session diff + write the entry; re-running this watchdog after dispatch should clear the alert.\"\n  printf '[%s] %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALERT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  printf '[%s] lesson-synthesis-watchdog: ALERT prod_touched=%s lessons=0\\n' \"$TS\" \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  echo \"$ALERT\" >&2\nfi","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh — Stage 2 of self-upgrade loop (BP-S43b-4 / KI-S43b-5).\n#\n# Detects sessions that touched production code without appending ≥1 lesson entry\n# to known-issues.md / best-practices.md / agent-notes.md. Advisory exit 0; emits\n# alert to stderr + log so the agent sees it before /clear or session-end.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1.\n# Stop hook (priority 2 — after self-awareness-aggregate, before promotion-cycle).\n# Decision basis: KI-S43b-5 (9-session dormant loop) + BP-S43b-4 promotion target.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nSA_DIR=\"$MEM_DIR/self-awareness\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.lesson-synthesis.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\n\n# Scope: count files touched in production tree (staged + unstaged + untracked).\n# git status --short gives one line per touched file regardless of state.\nPROD_TOUCHED=$(cd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" && git status --short -- packages/ apps/ 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n[[ \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || PROD_TOUCHED=0\n\n# Threshold: if 0 files touched, no lesson expected\nif [ \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" -lt 1 ]; then\n  printf '[%s] lesson-synthesis-watchdog: prod_touched=%s files (under threshold; skip)\\n' \\\n    \"$TS\" \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Were any of the lesson-target files updated in last 24h?\nKI_RECENT=$(find \"$SA_DIR/known-issues.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\nBP_RECENT=$(find \"$SA_DIR/best-practices.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\nNOTES_RECENT=$(find \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n\nLESSON_TOTAL=$(( KI_RECENT + BP_RECENT + NOTES_RECENT ))\n\nprintf '[%s] lesson-synthesis-watchdog: prod_touched=%s ki_recent=%s bp_recent=%s notes_recent=%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" \"$KI_RECENT\" \"$BP_RECENT\" \"$NOTES_RECENT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$LESSON_TOTAL\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  ALERT=\"lesson-synthesis-watchdog ALERT: production files touched = $PROD_TOUCHED but ZERO lesson entries (KI/BP/agent-notes) updated in last 24h. Self-upgrade loop Stage 2 dormant. Append ≥1 entry per BP-S43b-4 before next /clear.\"\n  printf '[%s] %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALERT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  printf '[%s] lesson-synthesis-watchdog: ALERT prod_touched=%s lessons=0\\n' \"$TS\" \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  echo \"$ALERT\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":44,"oldLines":7,"newStart":44,"newLines":7,"lines":["   \"$TS\" \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" \"$KI_RECENT\" \"$BP_RECENT\" \"$NOTES_RECENT\" >> \"$LOG\""," "," if [ \"$LESSON_TOTAL\" -eq 0 ]; then","-  ALERT=\"lesson-synthesis-watchdog ALERT: production files touched = $PROD_TOUCHED but ZERO lesson entries (KI/BP/agent-notes) updated in last 24h. Self-upgrade loop Stage 2 dormant. {"parentUuid":"04a24d1c-68b0-443e-822c-006aa05c256e","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_013mvBQusVPuqM9Q9TgTaCyL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01YUPDLWAkKje6hQCqsryCD6","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\memory-routing-audit.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# memory-routing-audit.sh — companion hook for HR-4 charter proposal\n# (`agent-workspace/proposals/memory-routing-tree.md`).\n#\n# STATUS: DRAFT — NOT yet wired into .claude/settings.json.\n# Ratification path: when human approves the HR-4 charter proposal, this hook\n# moves into the Stop chain (after lesson-synthesis-watchdog). Until then,\n# it stays unwired but smoke-testable for dry-run validation.\n#\n# Mechanism:\n#   1. Detect this session touched project work (production code or workspace\n#      memory): `git status --short -- packages/ apps/ agent-workspace/`\n#      non-empty.\n#   2. Detect user-memory dir was written in last 24h (mtime check on the\n#      MEMORY.md index in that dir).\n#   3. Detect agent-notes.md was NOT written in last 24h (project lesson loop\n#      bypassed).\n#   4. If 1 + 2 + 3 all true → ALERT (suggests project-substrate lesson\n#      misrouted to user-memory; per HR-4 proposal § Hard Rule 1).\n#\n# Modes:\n#   default → stderr ALERT + log; exit 0\n#   strict  → exit 2 when triple-trigger (STOCKFORGE_MEMORY_ROUTING_HARDBLOCK=1\n#             OR STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE=strict)\n#\n# User-memory dir is machine-local. Override the default path via\n# STOCKFORGE_USER_MEMORY_DIR env var; otherwise fall back to a heuristic.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.memory-routing-audit.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\n# Resolve user-memory dir. Default heuristic for the project owner's env;\n# override via STOCKFORGE_USER_MEMORY_DIR.\nUSER_MEM_DIR=\"${STOCKFORGE_USER_MEMORY_DIR:-/c/Users/PC/.ccs/instances/nathanleewindy/projects/C--htdocs-stockforge/memory}\"\nUSER_MEM_INDEX=\"$USER_MEM_DIR/MEMORY.md\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\n\n# Step 1: project work touched?\nPROD_TOUCHED=$(cd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" && git status --short -- packages/ apps/ agent-workspace/ 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n[[ \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || PROD_TOUCHED=0\n\nif [ \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" -lt 1 ]; then\n  printf '[%s] memory-routing-audit: prod_touched=0; skip\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Step 2: user-memory write in last 24h?\nUSER_MEM_FRESH=0\nif [ -f \"$USER_MEM_INDEX\" ]; then\n  if find \"$USER_MEM_INDEX\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then\n    USER_MEM_FRESH=1\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Also check any *.md inside the dir for recent writes (more robust than just index)\nUSER_MEM_RECENT_FILES=0\nif [ -d \"$USER_MEM_DIR\" ]; then\n  USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES=$(find \"$USER_MEM_DIR\" -name '*.md' -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n  [[ \"$USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES=0\nfi\nif [ \"$USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  USER_MEM_FRESH=1\nfi\n\n# Step 3: agent-notes.md fresh?\nNOTES_RECENT=$(find \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n[[ \"$NOTES_RECENT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || NOTES_RECENT=0\n\nprintf '[%s] memory-routing-audit: prod_touched=%s user_mem_fresh=%s user_mem_files=%s notes_recent=%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" \"$USER_MEM_FRESH\" \"$USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES\" \"$NOTES_RECENT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$USER_MEM_FRESH\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$NOTES_RECENT\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  ALERT=\"memory-routing-audit ALERT: project work touched (prod_touched=$PROD_TOUCHED files) AND user-memory written in last 24h (files=$USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES) AND agent-notes.md NOT written in last 24h. Per HR-4 proposal Hard Rule 1, project-substrate lessons must land in agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md, NOT user-memory dir. Verify recent user-memory writes are user-role/cross-project (allowed) vs project-substrate (must mirror to agent-notes.md).\"\n  printf '[%s] %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALERT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  printf '[%s] memory-routing-audit: ALERT triple-trigger\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  echo \"$ALERT\" >&2\n\n  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_MEMORY_ROUTING_HARDBLOCK:-0}\" = \"1\" ] || [ \"${STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE:-standard}\" = \"strict\" ]; then\n    printf '[%s] memory-routing-audit: HARD-BLOCK exit=2 (strict)\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit 0\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1932,"cache_read_input_tokens":175045,"output_tokens":3791,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1932,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":3791,"cache_read_input_tokens":175045,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1932,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1932},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CabzorFVQxdbjWKvPLYP4","type":"assistant","uuid":"90b6a89a-04b1-444e-b563-516063ffff08","timestamp":"2026-05-01T14:21:18.984Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

If 1 + 2 + 3 all true → ALERT (suggests project-substrate lesson\n#      misrouted to user-memory; per HR-4 proposal § Hard Rule 1).\n#\n# Modes:\n#   default → stderr ALERT + log; exit 0\n#   strict  → exit 2 when triple-trigger (STOCKFORGE_MEMORY_ROUTING_HARDBLOCK=1\n#             OR STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE=strict)\n#\n# User-memory dir is machine-local. Override the default path via\n# STOCKFORGE_USER_MEMORY_DIR env var; otherwise fall back to a heuristic.\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.memory-routing-audit.log\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\n# Resolve user-memory dir. Default heuristic for the project owner's env;\n# override via STOCKFORGE_USER_MEMORY_DIR.\nUSER_MEM_DIR=\"${STOCKFORGE_USER_MEMORY_DIR:-/c/Users/PC/.ccs/instances/nathanleewindy/projects/C--htdocs-stockforge/memory}\"\nUSER_MEM_INDEX=\"$USER_MEM_DIR/MEMORY.md\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\"\n\n# Step 1: project work touched?\nPROD_TOUCHED=$(cd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" && git status --short -- packages/ apps/ agent-workspace/ 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n[[ \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || PROD_TOUCHED=0\n\nif [ \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" -lt 1 ]; then\n  printf '[%s] memory-routing-audit: prod_touched=0; skip\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Step 2: user-memory write in last 24h?\nUSER_MEM_FRESH=0\nif [ -f \"$USER_MEM_INDEX\" ]; then\n  if find \"$USER_MEM_INDEX\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then\n    USER_MEM_FRESH=1\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Also check any *.md inside the dir for recent writes (more robust than just index)\nUSER_MEM_RECENT_FILES=0\nif [ -d \"$USER_MEM_DIR\" ]; then\n  USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES=$(find \"$USER_MEM_DIR\" -name '*.md' -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n  [[ \"$USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES=0\nfi\nif [ \"$USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  USER_MEM_FRESH=1\nfi\n\n# Step 3: agent-notes.md fresh?\nNOTES_RECENT=$(find \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n[[ \"$NOTES_RECENT\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || NOTES_RECENT=0\n\nprintf '[%s] memory-routing-audit: prod_touched=%s user_mem_fresh=%s user_mem_files=%s notes_recent=%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$PROD_TOUCHED\" \"$USER_MEM_FRESH\" \"$USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES\" \"$NOTES_RECENT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$USER_MEM_FRESH\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$NOTES_RECENT\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  ALERT=\"memory-routing-audit ALERT: project work touched (prod_touched=$PROD_TOUCHED files) AND user-memory written in last 24h (files=$USER_MEM_RECENT_FILES) AND agent-notes.md NOT written in last 24h. Per HR-4 proposal Hard Rule 1, project-substrate lessons must land in agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md, NOT user-memory dir. Verify recent user-memory writes are user-role/cross-project (allowed) vs project-substrate (must mirror to agent-notes.md).\"\n  printf '[%s] %s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$ALERT\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  printf '[%s] memory-routing-audit: ALERT triple-trigger\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  echo \"$ALERT\" >&2\n\n  if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_MEMORY_ROUTING_HARDBLOCK:-0}\" = \"1\" ] || [ \"${STOCKFORGE_HOOK_PROFILE:-standard}\" = \"strict\" ]; then\n    printf '[%s] memory-routing-audit: HARD-BLOCK exit=2 (strict)\\n' \"$TS\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 2\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"90b6a89a-04b1-444e-b563-516063ffff08","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"57277206-fd7d-4a87-a3f7-07d25ca4088b","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

user-memory bypassed project loop)\n  - user verbatim 2026-05-01 (S43b-EVIDENCE pivot): \"bạn lưu rất nhiều memory, note. chúng khiến hệ thống rất dễ lỗi liên quan đến llm\"\n  - agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md (HR-4 deferred to charter amendment)\ncompanion_artifacts:\n  - scripts/hooks/memory-routing-audit.sh (PROPOSED — not yet authored; would warn on user-memory writes during sessions touching packages/ or agent-workspace/)\nrelates_to:\n  - agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md (sibling charter — defines Tier 1/2/3 within project memory tree; does NOT cover project-vs-user-memory routing)\nsibling_proposals_in_dir:\n  - architecture-amendment.md\n  - drift-signals-amendment-DR-INTENT.md\n  - financial-data-protocol-amendment.md\n  - financial-data-protocol-amendment-VN.md\n  - invariants-amendment-VN.md\n  - provenance-protocol.md\n  - session-budgets-amendment.md\n---\n\n# Memory Routing Tree — PROPOSAL\n\n> **Status**: PROPOSAL (draft authored 2026-05-01 by agent during HR-4 harness recovery). Requires human ratification per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` Rule 1 (\"Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval\"). On approval: move to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-routing-tree.md` OR merge as new § into `memory-tiers.md`.\n\n## Purpose\n\nMake explicit where each kind of learned memory lands. Without this routing tree, the agent silently bypasses the project lesson-loop by writing project-scoped lessons into the user-memory directory — a failure mode user-detected at S43b-EVIDENCE (KI-S43b-5: 12+ entries accumulated as user-memory when they should have been `agent-notes.md`). Bypass starves the deterministic Stop-hook lesson-synthesis chain (HR-1 watchdog only inspects project memory tree), so patterns get re-discovered each session.\n\nThis proposal complements `memory-tiers.md` (which defines Tier 1/2/3 *within* the project memory tree) by adding **horizontal** scope routing across two surfaces:\n\n- **Project memory** = `agent-workspace/memory/` (version-controlled with the repo; deterministic hooks consume; promotion candidates flow to skills/charters)\n- **User memory** = `C:\\Users\\PC\\.ccs\\instances\\nathanleewindy\\projects\\C--htdocs-stockforge\\memory\\` (machine-local; cross-project / user-role; auto-loaded by harness; NOT version-controlled with repo)\n\n## Routing Decision Tree\n\nWhen the agent learns something worth remembering, route via this tree (top-down; first match wins):\n\n```\nQ1: Is this lesson specific to stockforge code, patterns, sessions, or substrate?\n    YES → agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md (project memory; canonical)\n    NO  → continue to Q2\n\nQ2: Is this about user (role / language / preferences) or cross-project (machine\n    paths / general Claude Code conventions / external service auth)?\n    YES → user-memory dir (e.g., user.md, language.md, machine_paths.md)\n    NO  → continue to Q3\n\nQ3: Is the lesson ambiguous (could fit either)?\n    YES → BOTH surfaces accept, but agent-notes.md is the canonical project copy\n          (so deterministic hooks see it; user-memory is the secondary \"nice to have\")\n    NO  → re-classify; this branch is unreachable for well-formed lessons\n```\n\n## Hard Rules (BINDING upon ratification)\n\n1. **NEVER write project-substrate lessons to user-memory.** Examples of project-substrate (always project memory):\n   - \"Stockforge bull sonnet timeout pattern\" → `agent-notes.md`\n   - \"RatioService bank schema requires separate spec\" → `agent-notes.md` + `decisions/`\n   - \"Phase 1 gatherer must wire compute services upstream\" → `agent-notes.md` + (eventually) `architecture.md`\n   - \"TA features wiring gap caused 0-grounded-points dogfood\" → `known-issues.md`\n2. **NEVER write user-role context to project memory.** Examples of user-role (always user-memory):\n   - \"User prefers Vietnamese for chat, English for code\"\n   - \"User uses /effort selectively across modes\"\n   - \"User's machine-local path is `C:\\htdocs\\...`\"\n3. **When ambiguous, project memory wins as canonical.** Reason: deterministic hooks (lesson-synthesis-watchdog, drift-rollup-daily, harness-recovery-dod-watchdog) only see project memory; user-memory writes are invisible to the harness self-upgrade loop.\n4. **Companion deterministic enforcement**: PROPOSED hook `scripts/hooks/memory-routing-audit.sh` — at Stop, if `git status --short -- packages/ apps/ agent-workspace/` non-empty AND user-memory dir mtime fresh (last 24h) AND no fresh write to `agent-notes.md` → ALERT (suggests project-substrate lesson was misrouted to user-memory).\n\n## Acceptance Process\n\n1. Human reviews this proposal in-place at `agent-workspace/proposals/memory-routing-tree.md`.\n2. If approved verbatim: move file to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-routing-tree.md` (drop `proposals/` prefix); set frontmatter `status: CHARTER` + `ratified_at: <date>` + `ratified_by: <user explicit>` + `ratifying_decision: D-NNN`. Optionally merge content as new § into `memory-tiers.md` to consolidate.\n3. If approved with edits: human edits in `proposals/`; agent applies post-edit move on confirmation.\n4. If rejected: file stays in `proposals/` as historical artifact; agent operates by current implicit convention until further guidance.\n\n## Drift / Risk Notes\n\n- This proposal increases governance surface (one more charter file). Mitigation: the rule is short, low-ambiguity, and addresses a user-detected failure mode. Net governance load is positive.\n- The companion hook `memory-routing-audit.sh` adds 1 to the Stop hook chain (currently 17 hooks post-HR-3/HR-8). Within tolerance.\n- Risk of over-routing: agent might over-classify lessons as project-substrate to satisfy the hook. Mitigation: BP-S43b-5 § \"how_to_apply\" already says when ambiguous, both surfaces acceptable.\n\n## Self-audit\n\n- Provenance: every claim above traces to a verbatim source (BP-S43b-5, KI-S43b-5, user prompt, agent-notes 2026-05-01).\n- I-S2 (every claim has source + as-of): proposal artifact carries `authored_at`; companion citations carry their own as-of via the referenced files.\n- I-S1 (no LLM math): no numbers computed; only counted (12+ user-memory entries) and that count is grep-able from the source files.\n- Charter immutability: this is a PROPOSAL, written to `agent-workspace/proposals/` per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` Rule 1. User-memory dir path overridable via `STOCKFORGE_USER_MEMORY_DIR` env var.","originalFile":"---\nstatus: PROPOSAL (draft — awaiting human ratification)\nauthored_at: 2026-05-01 (S43b-EVIDENCE harness recovery — HR-4)\nauthor: agent (autonomous draft per agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md Rule 1)\nratification_path: human approval → move to agent-workspace/constitution/memory-routing-tree.md (or merge into memory-tiers.md § \"Routing Decision Tree\")\nsource_evidence:\n  - agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md 2026-05-01 entry \"Project-Scoped Lessons Land in agent-notes.md, NOT user-memory dir\" (L-S43b-5)\n  - agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/best-practices.md § BP-S43b-5\n  - agent-workspace/memory/self-awareness/known-issues.md § KI-S43b-5 (lesson-synthesis Stage 2 dormant; user-memory bypassed project loop)\n  - user verbatim 2026-05-01 (S43b-EVIDENCE pivot): \"bạn lưu rất nhiều memory, note. chúng khiến hệ thống rất dễ lỗi liên quan đến llm\"\n  - agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md (HR-4 deferred to charter amendment)\ncompanion_artifacts:\n  - scripts/hooks/memory-routing-audit.sh (DRAFTED 2026-05-01 — 93 LOC; bash + POSIX; smoke-tested green; UNWIRED pending ratification; on approval add to .claude/settings.json Stop chain after lesson-synthesis-watchdog)\nrelates_to:\n  - agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md (sibling charter — defines Tier 1/2/3 within project memory tree; does NOT cover project-vs-user-memory routing)\nsibling_proposals_in_dir:\n  - architecture-amendment.md\n  - drift-signals-amendment-DR-INTENT.md\n  - financial-data-protocol-amendment.md\n  - financial-data-protocol-amendment-VN.md\n  - invariants-amendment-VN.md\n  - provenance-protocol.md\n  - session-budgets-amendment.md\n---\n\n# Memory Routing Tree — PROPOSAL\n\n> **Status**: PROPOSAL (draft authored 2026-05-01 by agent during HR-4 harness recovery). Requires human ratification per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` Rule 1 (\"Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval\"). On approval: move to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-routing-tree.md` OR merge as new § into `memory-tiers.md`.\n\n## Purpose\n\nMake explicit where each kind of learned memory lands. Without this routing tree, the agent silently bypasses the project lesson-loop by writing project-scoped lessons into the user-memory directory — a failure mode user-detected at S43b-EVIDENCE (KI-S43b-5: 12+ entries accumulated as user-memory when they should have been `agent-notes.md`). Bypass starves the deterministic Stop-hook lesson-synthesis chain (HR-1 watchdog only inspects project memory tree), so patterns get re-discovered each session.\n\nThis proposal complements `memory-tiers.md` (which defines Tier 1/2/3 *within* the project memory tree) by adding **horizontal** scope routing across two surfaces:\n\n- **Project memory** = `agent-workspace/memory/` (version-controlled with the repo; deterministic hooks consume; promotion candidates flow to skills/charters)\n- **User memory** = `C:\\Users\\PC\\.ccs\\instances\\nathanleewindy\\projects\\C--htdocs-stockforge\\memory\\` (machine-local; cross-project / user-role; auto-loaded by harness; NOT version-controlled with repo)\n\n## Routing Decision Tree\n\nWhen the agent learns something worth remembering, route via this tree (top-down; first match wins):\n\n```\nQ1: Is this lesson specific to stockforge code, patterns, sessions, or substrate?\n    YES → agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md (project memory; canonical)\n    NO  → continue to Q2\n\nQ2: Is this about user (role / language / preferences) or cross-project (machine\n    paths / general Claude Code conventions / external service auth)?\n    YES → user-memory dir (e.g., user.md, language.md, machine_paths.md)\n    NO  → continue to Q3\n\nQ3: Is the lesson ambiguous (could fit either)?\n    YES → BOTH surfaces accept, but agent-notes.md is the canonical project copy\n          (so deterministic hooks see it; user-memory is the secondary \"nice to have\")\n    NO  → re-classify; this branch is unreachable for well-formed lessons\n```\n\n## Hard Rules (BINDING upon ratification)\n\n1. **NEVER write project-substrate lessons to user-memory.** Examples of project-substrate (always project memory):\n   - \"Stockforge bull sonnet timeout pattern\" → `agent-notes.md`\n   - \"RatioService bank schema requires separate spec\" → `agent-notes.md` + `decisions/`\n   - \"Phase 1 gatherer must wire compute services upstream\" → `agent-notes.md` + (eventually) `architecture.md`\n   - \"TA features wiring gap caused 0-grounded-points dogfood\" → `known-issues.md`\n2. **NEVER write user-role context to project memory.** Examples of user-role (always user-memory):\n   - \"User prefers Vietnamese for chat, English for code\"\n   - \"User uses /effort selectively across modes\"\n   - \"User's machine-local path is `C:\\htdocs\\...`\"\n3. **When ambiguous, project memory wins as canonical.** Reason: deterministic hooks (lesson-synthesis-watchdog, drift-rollup-daily, harness-recovery-dod-watchdog) only see project memory; user-memory writes are invisible to the harness self-upgrade loop.\n4. **Companion deterministic enforcement**: PROPOSED hook `scripts/hooks/memory-routing-audit.sh` — at Stop, if `git status --short -- packages/ apps/ agent-workspace/` non-empty AND user-memory dir mtime fresh (last 24h) AND no fresh write to `agent-notes.md` → ALERT (suggests project-substrate lesson was misrouted to user-memory).\n\n## Acceptance Process\n\n1. Human reviews this proposal in-place at `agent-workspace/proposals/memory-routing-tree.md`.\n2. If approved verbatim: move file to `agent-workspace/constitution/memory-routing-tree.md` (drop `proposals/` prefix); set frontmatter `status: CHARTER` + `ratified_at: <date>` + `ratified_by: <user explicit>` + `ratifying_decision: D-NNN`. Optionally merge content as new § into `memory-tiers.md` to consolidate.\n3. If approved with edits: human edits in `proposals/`; agent applies post-edit move on confirmation.\n4. If rejected: file stays in `proposals/` as historical artifact; agent operates by current implicit convention until further guidance.\n\n## Drift / Risk Notes\n\n- This proposal increases governance surface (one more charter file). Mitigation: the rule is short, low-ambiguity, and addresses a user-detected failure mode. Net governance load is positive.\n- The companion hook `memory-routing-audit.sh` adds 1 to the Stop hook chain (currently 17 hooks post-HR-3/HR-8). Within tolerance.\n- Risk of over-routing: agent might over-classify lessons as project-substrate to satisfy the hook. Mitigation: BP-S43b-5 § \"how_to_apply\" already says when ambiguous, both surfaces acceptable.\n\n## Self-audit\n\n- Provenance: every claim above traces to a verbatim source (BP-S43b-5, KI-S43b-5, user prompt, agent-notes 2026-05-01).\n- I-S2 (every claim has source + as-of): proposal artifact carries `authored_at`; companion citations carry their own as-of via the referenced files.\n- I-S1 (no LLM math): no numbers computed; only counted (12+ user-memory entries) and that count is grep-able from the source files.\n- Charter immutability: this is a PROPOSAL, written to `agent-workspace/proposals/` per `agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md` Rule 1. Direct-write to `agent-workspace/constitution/*.md` is denied by `.claude/settings.json`.
